179. **Skill/XP gains must push `sendStats` / `sendSkills`** (`player/stats.rs`, TFS `addExperience`/`addSkillAdvance`/`addManaSpent`): Server-side `add_experience` / try counters are not visible until `0xA0`/`0xA1` are enqueued. Hardcoding `magic_level_percent: 0` and `percents: [0;7]` made bars look frozen even when manaspent/tries moved. Use `Player::percent_level` (TFS `getPercentLevel`) + `send_player_stats` after every positive XP share and `send_player_skills` after combat-skill tries. *(July 2026)*

180. **Exp popup + advance text are separate from stats packets** (`Creature::onGainExperience` `creature.cpp:771`, `addSkillAdvance`/`addManaSpent`/`addExperience` in 772 `player.cpp`): Kill XP needs `sendAnimatedText` with `TEXTCOLOR_WHITE_EXP` (215) at the gainer's tile — not only `0xA0`. Skill/magic/level ups need `MESSAGE_EVENT_ADVANCE` (0x13): "You advanced in sword fighting.", "You advanced to magic level N.", "You advanced from Level A to Level B.". Wire via `broadcast_experience_popup` / `send_player_advance_message` / `notify_*_tries_gained`. *(July 2026)*

181. **`function="onEquipItem"` is native C++, not Lua** (`movement.cpp` `MoveEvent::loadFunction` / `EquipItem`): XML `onEquipItem`/`onDeEquipItem` set `equipFunction` to C++ ability apply/remove — our loader previously registered a no-op Lua stub, so boots of haste (`abilities.speed=20`) never changed walk speed. Fix: call `GameWorld::apply_equip_item_abilities` / `remove_equip_item_abilities` from `fire_on_player_equip`/`deequip` (and login hydrate). Store equip deltas in `var_speed` / `var_skills` / `var_stats` (TFS `changeSpeed`/`setVarSkill`/`setVarStats`); walk uses `speed + var_speed` + condition Speed; combat/stats use `Player::skill_level` / `effective_max_*`. Do not bake equip into `base_speed` — `recompute_speed_from_conditions` would wipe it. **Inactive rings** have no abilities — abilities live on the **active** (post-`transformEquipTo`) type; apply from post-transform id (TFS reads pre-transform `ItemType`, which is empty for this pack). Always `set_item_ability(true)` after equip so deequip reverses (TFS only sets the flag in the non-transform branch). XML `duration` is seconds → schedule ms `× 1000`. Regen ticks in `process_equipment_regeneration` (~1000 ms cadence). Decay expiry uses `strip_equip_abilities_keep_type` (decay owns the type change, not `transformDeEquipTo`). Stealth ring: empty `0x8E` outfit while invisible. *(`equip_abilities.rs`, July 2026)*

182. **Player death must always queue a victim stats grant** (`death.rs` / `player.cpp:2153`): TFS clears blessings then always `sendStats()`/`sendSkills()`, even when `deathLosePercent = 0` (or bless-reduced loss floors to 0). Gating the victim `XpShareGrant` on `lose > 0` skipped `send_player_stats` and left cleared blessings (and skill loss) invisible to the client until relog. Always push a zero-amount victim grant; also `send_player_skills` after death. *(`death.rs`, `game_world_lifecycle.rs`, July 2026)*

183. **PC-3a spell conditions — shared mapper + heal/dispel order** (`ConditionApplySpec`, `active_condition_from_apply_spec`, `aoe.rs`): Lua `ConditionBuilder` fields must cross the mutation seam as a full spec (not just `ctype`/`ticks`) or light/haste/poison cycle/outfit are silently dropped. Map to `ConditionData::{Light,Speed,Damage,Outfit,Generic,Regeneration}` + `timer_rounds_left` from ms ticks (`ProcessSkills` ~1000 ms). Do **not** use `CombatParams.dispel` exclusive early-return for Lua AoE — healing spells set DISPEL(paralyze) *and* heal; apply damage/heal first, then conditions, then dispel (mirrors `postCombatEffects`). `combat:addCondition` and `creature:addCondition` share one mapper. *(`pc3a-spell-gaps.md` Phases 2–4, July 2026)*

184. **Condition apply must push client notifies** (`on_condition_started`/`ended`, Phase 4b): Server-side haste/light/invis/manashield without `sendIcons` / `changeLight` / empty-outfit invis looks broken on the client. Mirror TFS `Condition*::start/endCondition` + `Player::onAdd/onEndCondition`. Light uses `Player::internal_light` max'd with `items_light` (`Player::getCreatureLight`). Call from every add/remove/expire site (AoE, Lua, ProcessSkills) — do not only announce speed. Progressive light dim during duration still deferred. *(`game_world_chat.rs`, `aoe.rs`, `process_skills.rs`, July 2026)*

185. **`announce_creature_speed` must use protocol wire id, not SlotMap ffi** (`game_world_spectators.rs`): `0x8F` `SendCreatureSpeed` updates the creature the client knows by **guid** (players) / `wire_id` (monsters). Using `cid.data().as_ffi()` made haste/strong haste look like a no-op — client ignored the packet. Also: do not bake `ConditionData::Speed` into `base.speed` *and* re-sum conditions in `creature_effective_speed_for_step` (double-count). Effective speed = `base_speed + var_speed + condition deltas`. *(July 2026)*

186. **772 invisibility `0x8E` must use codec `AddOutfit`, not 1098 `write_outfit`** (`send_creature_outfit` / `encode_creature_outfit`): Phase 4b `announce_player_change_visible` (utana vid) broadcast empty outfit via `outgoing_extra::send_creature_outfit`, which always wrote 1098 layout (addons + mount). 7.72 `AddOutfit` has neither — extra bytes desync the client → immediate disconnect/debug. Fix: `ProtocolCodec::encode_creature_outfit` → `Codec772::write_outfit` / `Codec1098::write_outfit`. Same path for condition outfit + idle invis break. *(July 2026)*

187. **Invisible players vs monsters + floor-change outfit** (`can_see_creature`, `AddCreature`): (1) `can_see_creature` wrongly applied `Player::canSeeCreature` to *all* viewers — monsters could "see" invisible players. Base `Creature::canSeeCreature` hides invisibles unless `see_invisible`; only players override to still see invisible players. Also gate acquire (`crnonpl.cc:2514`) and `monster_is_opponent`. (2) Floor-change map rebuild used full `outfit` on AddCreature → client reappeared; use empty outfit while `CONDITION_INVISIBLE` (same as `0x8E` change-visible). *(July 2026)*

188. **Monster XML `range="1"` must be Victim, not Actor** (`default_shape_for_node`): Gate was `range > 1`, so melee-range spells (`ghoul`/`ghost`/`stalker` lifedrain, etc.) became `SpellShape::Actor` and hit the caster. 772 `ghoul.mon` is `Victim (1,0,0) -> Damage(LIFEDRAIN)`. Fix: `range > 0` (or `target="1"`). ~24 lifedrain attacks were affected. Self-cast Actor remains correct for healing / self-haste defenses. *(July 2026)*

189. **PC-3a Phase 5 conjure — do not reimplement `conjureItem` in Rust** (`functions.lua` `Player:conjureItem`): Fill Lua helpers only — `getMana`/`addMana`/`addManaSpent` (mutation + `magic_increase`), `ItemType:getCharges`, `item:hasAttribute` (bitflag vs `ItemAttrFlags`), `item:transform` (in-place type/subtype + inventory/container/tile notify), plus unblockers `Group:hasFlag` + `PlayerFlag_HasInfiniteMana` + `ITEM_ATTRIBUTE_*`. Hands-only path uses `remove`+`addItem(slot)`; `transform` is for the non-hands branch. Dual-hand second conjure is the only path that calls mana helpers after the spell system already deducted the first cost. *(`pc3a-spell-gaps.md` Phase 5, July 2026)*

190. **PC-3a Phases 6–8 combat params / callbacks**: `CREATEITEM`/`NODAMAGE`/`DISTANCEEFFECT` must be copied onto `CombatExecuteRequest` — storing on `CombatDef` alone is a no-op. CREATEITEM remaps persistent→PvP and NoPvP world/zone→NoPvP field IDs (`combatTileEffects`); place via `internal_add_item_to_tile` (same placement path as monster `SpellImpact::Field` / `CreateField`). Event callbacks (`TARGETCREATURE`/`TARGETTILE`) run in Lua after `call_combat_execute` using the same area offsets as damage (avoid game→Lua re-entry from `aoe.rs`). Diagonal `extArea` is a second matrix: when both caster→center dx/dy ≠ 0, use NW=raw / NE=mirror / SW=flip / SE=transpose on relative offsets. Field rune scripts need minimal `Tile(pos):hasProperty(BLOCKSOLID)` before execute. *(`pc3a-spell-gaps.md` Phases 6–8, July 2026)*

191. **PC-3a Gaps 5–6 — variant params + tile ground type + rune keys**: Instant `hasParams` must build `VARIANT_STRING` (4), not position — otherwise `utevo res` / find person / levitate never see `variant:getString()`. Attach `getString`/`getNumber`/`getPosition` via metatable (combat still reads table `.type`/`.pos`). Map ground is `Option<u16>` type id — Lua `getGround()` returns `ItemTypeRef` so `:getId()` works. Rune callbacks must key `rune:{id}` (words empty); `player_use_item_ex` dispatches `fire_on_cast_rune` before container-open. Summon flags/`manacost` parse from monster XML; `Game.createMonster` reuses `find_and_place_creature_tfs` without a spawn slot. *(`pc3a-spell-gaps.md` Gaps 5–6, July 2026)*

192. **Monster XML `<summons>` are 772 CASTING spells, not a separate think** (`crnonpl.cc:2568–2741`, `magic.cc` `TSummonImpact`): Do not port TVP `onThink` summon-after-attacks as a side path. Parse `<summons>` → merge into `MonsterSpell` with `SpellImpact::Summon` + `SpellShape::Origin` r=0 so they ride IdleStimulus CASTING (ToDo wakeup). `TSummonImpact` is `handleField`-only — Origin/Destination must call `handleField` (not only victims). Gates: `Master == 0`, `SummonedCreatures < Maximum`, same delay/flee as other spells; place via `SearchSummonField` + `CreateMonster` with `EFFECT_ENERGY` (11). Aggressive → needs a combat target. *(July 2026)*

193. **Summon/createMonster must `broadcast_creature_appear`** (`spawn_lifecycle.rs`): Placing on the map + `monster_on_creature_appear_self` (AI only) is not enough — spectators need `0x6A` AddCreature like `spawn_monster`. Without it the client gets magic-effect / later Move for an unknown creature id and disconnects. Order: appear_self → `broadcast_creature_appear` → effect. Same fix for `lua_script_create_monster` (player `utevo res`). *(July 2026)*

194. **Summon rebind: `Combat.Following` ≠ `follow_target.is_some()` for monsters** (`crnonpl.cc:2397–2405`, `idle_stimulus.rs` `monster_idle_summon_lifecycle`): Decompile sets summon Target to `Master->AttackDest` unless `Combat.Following`. In Rust, player follow-mode sets `follow_target`; monster chase also sets `follow_target` while attacking. Mapping Following ← `follow_target.is_some()` for **monster** masters made giant-spider summons Target=0 → fall back to Master (the spider), so they never attacked the player. Fix: player master → Following = `follow_target.is_some()`; monster master → Following always false, inherit `attack_target`. *(July 2026)*

195. **Own-summon kick matches `KickCreature` push-first** (`crnonpl.cc:3057–3080`, `monster_push.rs`): Do not special-case `blocker.Master == kicker` to always kill. Decompile tries N/S/W/E shove for every pushable monster (including own summons); kill only when no escape tile (`Kein Platz zum Verschieben => Töten` → `EXHAUSTED`). An earlier always-trample path was reverted. *(July 2026)*

196. **Summon/field CASTING parity gaps** (`crnonpl.cc` / `magic.cc` / `info.cc`): (1) `CreateMonster` reparents summon-of-summon by walking Master until wild/player (`crnonpl.cc:2012–2028`) — do this in `monster_create_summon`, not the CASTING `Master==0` gate. (2) After `SearchSummonField`, call `SearchFreeField` r=2 and keep coords on failure (`crnonpl.cc:3169` / `info.cc:761`). (3) Aggressive Origin/Destination/Angle tiles skip PZ (`ExecuteCircleSpell` `magic.cc:475–477`); Actor aggressive+caster PZ early-returns (`:406`). (4) `poisonfield`/`firefield`/`energyfield` → `SpellImpact::Field { field_type }` + `CreateField` (`FieldPossible`, delete MAGICFIELD, `internal_add_item_to_tile` with 1490/1487/1491). *(July 2026)*

197. **Item Expire ≡ TFS decay, not a second system** (`map.cc` CronExpire / `operate.cc` ProcessCronSystem ↔ `game.cpp` startDecay/checkDecay): Decompile `Expire`+`TotalExpireTime`+`ExpireTarget`+`ExpireStop` maps 1:1 to `items.xml` `duration`/`decayto`/`stopduration` (seconds in XML, ms on the instance). Keep TFS domain names; schedule with a deadline map (Cron-like), not TFS 4-bucket lists. Phases 1–5 done (typed fields, general apply, stopduration, login Pending, Empty, depot/`showduration`). Phase 6 still: fold corpse/splash ad-hoc schedulers. Plan: `tasks/decay-system-plan.md`. *(July 2026)*

198. **Decay apply is location-dispatched, not equipment-only** (`decay_apply.rs`): `start_decay` / `can_decay` / `internal_decay_item` mirror TFS domain; cron pops deadlines then `match` on `Cylinder` (equip strip → transform/remove; tile/container notify). `LuaMutation::ItemDecay` → `start_decay`. Do not leave expired `DecayEntry`s without an apply path — corpses/fields scheduled in Phase 1 were silently dropped until Phase 2. *(July 2026)*

199. **`stopduration` is pause/resume via `change_item_type`** (plan §4.3): On type change, if old was timing → `stop_decay` for remaining; if new has `stop_time` keep duration unscheduled; else if new has `decay_time` seed/resume `start_decay`. Cylinder add owns `start_decay`; full destroy owns `cancel_item_decay`. Equip/lua transforms must not clear duration on deequip. *(July 2026)*

200. **Login Pending decay + save remaining** (`load.rs` / `item_blob.rs` / `game_world_save.rs`): Blob parse maps any non-zero DecayingState → `Pending`. Login hydrate bypasses cylinder add, so call `restart_pending_decay_for_player` after load — `start_decay` reads Duration raw ms. Player save must write `DecayManager::remaining_ms` (not schedule-time snapshot) via `write_item_blob_with_duration`. House `tile_store` apply still missing — reuse the restart helper when it lands. *(July 2026)*

201. **Expire Empty before transform** (`operate.cc` `Empty` / `ProcessCronSystem`): Not in stock TFS `internalDecayItem`. Before stage change: close viewers; if next target is a container, keep up to its `max_items`; else remainder 0. Corpse (`corpsetype`): **delete** excess. Non-corpse: **move** excess to parent cylinder. Final vanish with remainder 0 clears loot (players must loot before last stage). *(July 2026)*

202. **TVP `can_decay` + showduration look**: Refuse uniqueId, actionId ∈ `[1000,2000]`, and items inside depot chest/locker unless `itemsDecayInsideDepots` (default `false` in `config.lua`). Look `showduration` uses scheduler remaining when `DecayState::True`, else raw Duration — do not rely on unset wall-clock `duration_timestamp`. *(July 2026)*

203. **Combat `setCallback` must snapshot the Lua function** (`luascript.cpp` `LuaScriptInterface::getEvent`): Spell scripts all define the same global `onGetFormulaValues`. TFS `setCallback` → `getEvent` stores a registry id and **clears the global**. Looking up the global name at `combat:execute` time makes every spell use whichever formula was loaded last (often UE → ~900 dmg on energy strike). Fix: store `mlua::Function` in `CombatCallback` at `setCallback`, set global to `Nil`, invoke the snapshotted function. Test: `set_callback_snapshots_function_so_later_global_overwrite_is_ignored`. *(July 2026)*

204. **Idle `ShouldSleep` must ignore wild monsters** (`crnonpl.cc:2500-2556`): C++ skips non-player-controlled monsters **before** `CanSeeFloor`. Rust set `should_sleep = false` for any same-floor creature first, so dense spawn packs never slept (each rat kept every other rat awake) → ToDo/IdleStimulus thrash → `Delay >= 1000` MoveCreatures skip. Fix: filter wild monsters/NPCs first; use `CanSeeFloor` (`cr.hh:576`) for sleep; multifloor 12×12 search (XY chain spans Z); on sleep clear ToDo + wakeup (no `ToDoStart`). Tests: `idle_acquire_wild_bystander_does_not_prevent_sleep`, `idle_acquire_upstairs_player_keeps_awake_without_target`. *(July 2026)*

205. **Gameloop lag — O(1) item parent + cron heap + dense TShortway** (live `todo_us`~275ms / `cron_us`~4s): (1) Decompile Cron applies via live `Object` + `getContainer()` (`map.cc` `GetObjectCoordinates`) — never scans the map. Rust `resolve_item_parent_cylinder` used `find_item_position` over ~7.8M tiles. Fix: `Item.parent: Option<Cylinder>` maintained at place/remove hubs (OTBM/`load`/`depot`/tile/inv/container); resolve is O(1). (2) `DecayManager` was `HashMap::retain` each cron fire — replace with deadline min-heap + lazy cancel (772 `TCronEntry`/`CronCheck` shape). (3) `TShortway` used per-pathfind `HashMap<Position,_>` — densify to `±(Visible+1)` index buffer matching C++ `matrix` (`cract.cc`); keep expand/heuristic/neighbor parity. Unused `ItemPosition` enum removed in favor of `Cylinder`. *(July 2026)*

206. **Game-thread lag is admission + amplification, not the ToDo heap itself** (`main.cc` `LaunchGame`/`AdvanceGame`, `crmain.cc` `MoveCreatures`, `map.cc` Cron heap): Keep the reference all-due ToDo/Cron drains and structural tie order; arbitrary budgets/jitter change outcomes. First remove avoidable work: never await login DB I/O on `&mut GameWorld`; bound command/output channels so a biased drain cannot starve beats; use indexed decay cancellation like `CronDelete` rather than far-future stale keys; batch container-expiry bookkeeping; prebuild monster spell data; reuse dense path/search scratch; implement `Execute` as a Rust loop, not recursion (tail-call elimination is not guaranteed). Decay must use the round/cron clock, not lag-skipped `ServerMilliseconds`, because the reference advances `RoundNr` while `MoveCreatures` is skipped. Full audit: `docs/GAME_LOOP_DECAY_IDLE_TODO_PERFORMANCE_AUDIT.md`. *(July 2026)*

207. **Idle CASTING RNG stream is glibc parity only** (`crnonpl.cc:2521-2667`, `magic.cc:776`): `monster_idle_apply_spell_impact` already uses `parity_random` / `parity_rand_mod` for delay gates, flee rolls, damage/heal variance — not `StdRng`. The per-pass `StdRng::from_entropy()` scratch was dead weight (unused `_rng` param); removing it does not change outcomes. Spell lists and `<defenses>` nodes are merged once at spawn in `combat_from_monster_type`; talk lines use `Arc<[String]>` with RNG gate before borrow. *(July 2026 — audit IDLE-1)*

208. **Game-loop audit remediation landed (GL/DEC/IDLE/TODO)** (`docs/GAME_LOOP_DECAY_IDLE_TODO_PERFORMANCE_AUDIT.md`): (1) Login load is Tokio-spawned; game thread only applies `PlayerLoaded` when `conn_id` is still current (`MAX_CONCURRENT_LOGIN_LOADS=8`). (2) Dual-lane `GameCmdTx` — bounded game packets (`8192` / `64` per turn) + unbounded control; beats serviced after command budget. (3) Output: game-thread `OutboundTx` map + bounded per-conn queues with slow-client shed (GL-3). (4) Decay: parent-cylinder equip expiry, batched empty, iterative destroy, indexed heap (`CronDelete` shape), `DecayClockModel::RoundNumber` for 772 so lag-skipped movement does not freeze cron. (5) Idle: prebuilt spells, `TShortwayScratch` on `GameWorld`, scratch Vecs for think/skills/spectators. (6) ToDo `Execute` is an explicit loop with iteration guard. Do **not** add per-beat ToDo/cron caps (TODO-2) until load-test of this path. *(July 2026)*

209. **GL-3 soft byte cap must not reject empty-queue floor-change bursts** (`0x64` / `emit_teleport_move_packet`): Soft-capping at 256 KiB with shed-on-Full disconnected OTClient when changing onto a dense monster floor (map description + creature appears exceed the cap in one flush). Fix: soft cap (2 MiB) only when `queued_bytes > 0`; hard shed at 8 MiB; `try_send` returns the batch on error so Full re-queues instead of dropping packets. *(July 2026)*

210. **TShortwayScratch generation must reset `expand_next`/`parent`** (IDLE-2): `touch_path_cell` only stamped `path_gen` and left linked-list open-set pointers from the previous search. After a floor-change, many monsters wake and repath on the shared scratch — the first `expand()` of a new search set `expand_head` to a stale `expand_next`, corrupting chase paths / hanging the expand loop. That shows up as client desync when monsters start targeting. Fix: on gen change, reset waylength/heuristic/parent/`expand_next`; clear expand links in FillMap outer pass. *(July 2026)*

211. **Ctrl+C hang after “flushing online players”** (`run_server` / `flush_online_players_to_db`): The log fired when `Shutdown` was *sent*, not when flush ran. If the game thread is wedged in a sync beat (pathfinding storm), `Shutdown` never drains and the process never exits. Separately, `JoinSet::spawn` of DB saves from a `LocalSet` task can deadlock when workers are parked in `LocalSet::run_until`. Fix: honest log; drive saves with `block_in_place` + `Handle::block_on`; second Ctrl+C or 10s timeout force-exits. *(July 2026)*

212. **Logout must drop `OutboundTx` in the registry (close TCP)** (`ProtocolGame::logout` / `disconnect`): GL-3 kept a clone of `OutboundTx` in `out_registry`. Logout only removed the game-thread sink, so the writer task never exited and TCP stayed open while the creature was already gone — OTClient desynced until the client timed out (~10s). Fix: flush conn pending, then drop sink + registry entry (writer exits → `shutdown`). Wire `0x14` through `player_logout_allowed` before `PlayerDisconnect`. *(July 2026)*

213. **GL-3 outbound / ingress review fixes**: (1) Never drop flushed batches on missing sink / `Closed` / disconnect-flush failure — re-queue (and shed on `Closed`). Mirror sink from `OutRegistry` via `ensure_output_sink`. (2) `GameLaneFull` sheds the TCP connection instead of silently skipping packets. (3) `onLogout` returning `false` cancels logout (`EventDispatcher::on_logout -> bool`). (4) Disconnect player save uses `block_in_place` + `block_on` like shutdown flush (LocalSet-safe). *(July 2026)*

214. **GL-1 login session close + DEC-3 stop_decay units**: Logout with no `conn_to_creature` (async load window) still queues `PlayerDisconnect`. `PlayerLoadFailed` / apply / bad payload close the outbound sink (TCP). `stop_decay` returns **item ms** after clock conversion — returning raw rounds broke `change_item_type` stopduration on 772. *(July 2026)*

215. **`block_in_place` panics on LocalSet game loop**: Disconnect/shutdown saves must not call `block_in_place` / nested `block_on` — `LocalSet` disables blocking and panics with "can call blocking only when running on the multi-threaded runtime". Use `tokio::spawn` (logout fire-and-forget) or `JoinSet::spawn` + `.await` (shutdown flush yields to other workers). *(July 2026)*

216. **Todo execute guard + DEC-3 remaining-ms readers**: (1) `MAX_TODO_EXECUTE_ITERATIONS` must `todo_start_from_action(+1)` (or unlock if empty) — otherwise `next_wakeup` was already taken and `todo.locked` stalls idle forever. (2) Look/save must use `item_decay_remaining_ms` (`decay_clock_now` + round→ms); `remaining_ms(..., server_ms)` under 772 `RoundNumber` reads as ~0 and corrupts duration. *(July 2026)*

217. **OBS-1 Phase 0: windowed fixed histograms, not per-action INFO** (`docs/GAME_LOOP_DECAY_IDLE_TODO_PERFORMANCE_AUDIT.md`): Game-thread `GameObs` / `FixedHistogram` records beat lateness, subsystem µs, ToDo due/stale/lateness, path expand/wall, idle passes, decay due, outbound queued bytes — emitted every 10s at `target=tfs_obs` (`game_obs_summary`). Command age is game-thread visibility age (pending Instant stamp). Do not add TODO-2 caps until live baselines (`docs/GAME_LOOP_OBS_BASELINES.md`) + load-test say otherwise. *(July 2026)*

218. **IDLE-3 sector order replaces SlotMap-key sort** (`crmain.cc` `TFindCreatures::getNext`): Spectator / idle-acquire / move-stimulus fan-out now walk 16×16 sectors (blocky outer, blockx inner) via `collect_spectators_sector_order`, with generation-stamped dedup across Z / old+new. Within-sector order is tile-list order (exact LIFO `NextChainCreature` would need per-sector chains). Beat timer uses `interval_at(now+beat)` so the first advance waits one beat like `LaunchGame`. Full-scale tests cover command-flood lateness, SlowClient shed, login+decay+ToDo continuity, 2k decay burst, spell idle burst, 256-path scratch storm, randomized stale ToDo. GL-4 indexes and TODO-2 still deferred. *(July 2026)*

219. **Open ground corpse + walk = full-map scan per step** (`player.cpp` `autoCloseContainers` / `Thing::getPosition`): Every successful player step calls `auto_close_containers_for_player` → distance check. Inventory bags early-out; **ground** windows used `map.find_item_position` (scan every chunk×tile). With a dense map that is hundreds of ms–1s **per step**, billed as `todo_us`, then `Delay >= 1000` skips `MoveCreatures`. C++ walks the parent cylinder for position. Fix: `script_item_position` / `Item.parent` (O(1)) in auto-close + inventory notify + house container lookup. *(July 2026)*

220. **Monster XML `shooteffect="poison"` ≠ poison arrow; TFS `radius` ≠ 772 disc radius** (`tools.cpp` `shootTypeNames`, `giantspider.mon` `Destination (7, 15, 0, 0)`, `combat.cpp:setupArea`): (1) `"poison"`/`"earth"` → `CONST_ANI_POISON` (15); `"poisonarrow"` → `CONST_ANI_POISONARROW` (6). Collapsing them made giant spiders fire poison-arrow missiles. (2) TFS `AreaCombat::setupArea(R)` includes rings `1..=R` (center = ring 1); 772 `ExecuteCircleSpell` uses rings `0..=R`. So XML `radius="1"` is center-only (`setupArea(1)`), not the plus-shaped `disc_offsets(1)`. Store `xml_radius - 1` for Origin/Destination after shape detection. *(July 2026)*

221. **Utevo lux “no light” — CONDITION_LIGHT color defaulted to 0** (`light.lua` sets LEVEL only; 772 `GetCreatureLight` `info.cc:1238-1281`): Skill-only brightness packs to color **215**. Sending level=6 color=0 is black light (invisible). Fix: when applying Light with `level > 0 && color == 0`, use 215. Also map Lua `COMBAT_NONE` (0) → `Undefined` and skip the damage arm for condition-only buffs. *(July 2026)*

222. **In-combat logout was set but never gated** (`BlockLogout` / `LogoutPossible` / `CheckState`): Combat wrote `earliest_logout_round` (772) while `player_logout_allowed` only checked `CONDITION_INFIGHT` (TFS) — melee never applied Infight, so logout succeeded mid-fight and swords never showed. Fix: gate on `earliest_logout_round > round_nr`; apply Infight alongside `BlockLogout`; `ICON_SWORDS` from Infight **or** pending logout round; delays from `config.lua` `pzLocked` (ms → rounds) / `whiteSkullTime` (secs). Defaults 60000 / 900 match 772 `BlockLogout(60)` / `(900)`. *(July 2026)*

223. **Swords icon enqueued but not flushed to client** (`0xA2` / game loop): `BlockLogout` → `send_player_icons` queued into `pending_outgoing`, but the loop only flushed on beat advance — command-only turns left icons sitting until the next alarm (and lagged beats made it worse). Fix: flush outgoing after every command batch when no beat is ready; `CheckState`-style `client_icons` OldState; monster `Attack` + player damage paths also call `player_block_logout_infight`. *(July 2026)*

224. **Getting hit did not set in-fight** (`Attack` Target->BlockLogout): Logout lock was applied to the **attacker** in `combat_execute_with_stimulus`, so monster→player was a no-op (non-player attacker). Melee arm also skipped the victim lock that ranged had. Fix: when offensive combat has an attacker and the target is a player, call `player_block_logout_infight(target, false)` (772 `crcombat.cc:602`). *(July 2026)*

225. **Retarget cleared attack-speed exhaust** (`SetAttackDest` / `crcombat.cc:438`): Rust zeroed `earliest_attack_ms` on successful retarget, mistaking 772 `LatestAttackTime = 0` (delayed `StopAttack` flag) for `EarliestAttackTime`. That let a second click on another monster strike immediately. Fix: leave `earliest_attack_ms` alone; `todo_attack_delay_ms` already waits the remaining exhaust. *(July 2026)*

226. **PZ lock only on melee hit, not on monster target acquire** (`AttackStimulus`): 772 locks via idle walk `SetAttackDest(Target, false)` → `Target->AttackStimulus` (`crnonpl.cc:2778-2784`, `crcombat.cc:433`) — **not** on `Target = …` / `selectTarget`. Fist>0 → ATTACKING (no melee-range gate); same-dest early-out skips re-stimulus. Rust wrongly fired from `selectTarget` and gated ATTACKING on melee/ranged. Fix: follow-only on select/summon Target assign; `monster_idle_maybe_enter_attacking` owns SetAttackDest + AttackStimulus. *(July 2026)*

206. **Map/grid performance audit — per-move fan-out amplification** (`docs/MAP_GRID_PERFORMANCE_PARITY_AUDIT.md`, July 2026): Companion to `MAP_SYSTEM_AUDIT.md` (correctness #1–#9, Phases 1–3 ✅). The correctness pass left **amplification** as the scaling ceiling: (1) every creature step does **4 sort+dedup passes** for spectator fan-out (`walk/mod.rs:1059-1062` + `monster_events.rs:110-132`) — at 1000 movers that's ~4000 sorts/sec on top of packet emission; 772 `TFindCreatures::getNext` (`crmain.cc:101-144`) walks 16×16 blocks once with no sort (linked-list order is fan-out order). Fix: single-pass `collect_spectators_dedup` with `FxHashSet`, one sort at end. (2) `find_item_position` is a full-world scan (every chunk × 4096 slots) — 772 `Object` carries a container parent pointer (`map.hh:64`), so item position is O(1). Fix: reverse `ItemId → Position` index maintained at tile add/remove. (3) `remove_creature` scans ALL creatures to find summons on every death — 772/TFS keep `summons` on the master (`creature.h`). Fix: `summons: SmallVec<[CreatureId; 2]>` on master, drain on death. (4) `scan_min_terrain_waypoints` does 441 `get_tile` hash lookups per pathfinding attempt — 772 `TShortway::FillMap` (`cract.cc:80-138`) direct-indexes the sector array. Fix: cache `&Chunk` across inner loop, pass tile ref to `ground_cost`. (5) Chunk granularity 4× coarser than 772's 16×16 creature blocks (audit #5, Phase 4) — over-collects 16× and drives the sort amplification in (1). Fix: separate `CreatureBlockIndex` with 16×16 blocks walked row-major. (6) LOS / throw loops re-hash `ChunkKey` per tile — cache `&Chunk` across line. (7) OTBM load allocates 3 `Vec::new()` per tile (300k allocs for 100k tiles) — switch to `SmallVec<[T; 2]>` (no alloc for empty tiles). (8) `TShortway` allocates 529-cell `Vec` per reverse path search — pool the buffer on `GameWorld`, `clear()` between uses. Phased plan A–E in the audit doc; Phase E (P2–P3 tuning) deferred until production profiling. *(July 2026)*

227. **DistanceAttack defense is attacker-shield side effects only** (`crcombat.cc:809-811`): Rust wrongly subtracted target defense+armor via `melee_damage_after_defense_and_armor` on ranged hits. 772 passes full `GetAttackDamage` into `Damage`; calls `GetDefendDamage` only when **attacker** `Shield != NONE` and discards the return (wearout/learning). Armor stays inside Physical `Damage`. Comments that said \"defender has a shield\" were wrong. *(July 2026 — PC-3a polish)*

228. **Scripted distance ammo uses Lua `onUseWeapon`, not hardcoded specials** (`weapons.cpp:365-369,485`, `burst_arrow.lua`): Wands/rods were already Lua *data* (`WandDef`); burst is Lua *behavior*. Capture `function weapon.onUseWeapon` like spells; `DistanceWeaponDef.has_on_use` + runtime callback registry. DistanceAttack skips native primary/poison when scripted, then after the missile calls `fire_on_use_weapon` (hit → `VARIANT_NUMBER`, miss → `VARIANT_POSITION` at drop). Do not hardcode item 2546 disc AoE — data-pack Combat area/formula owns the outcome. *(July 2026)*

229. **`COMBAT_FORMULA_SKILL` keeps TFS API shape; 772 magnitude is one ProbeValue** (`combat.cpp:120-129`, `crskill.cc:535`, `crcombat.cc:220`): 772 rolls `GetAttackDamage`-shaped ClassicProbe once (`772.lua` `damageTuning`), applies `×maxa+maxb`, returns `(v,v)` so AoE does not uniform-roll over a ceiling. 1098 Modern keeps TFS `getMaxWeaponDamage` + `[minb, hi]`. Tier-2 `getWeaponDamage` hooks apply when registered. *(July 2026)*

230. **Magic `computeDamage` must read `formulas.spell`, not hardcoded 2/3** (`magic.cc:776`, `772.lua`): Native `CreatureRef:computeDamage` / `computeHealing` / `computeSkillDamage` + `getSpellCoeff` use `MechanicsProfile.spell_coeff` (and Tier-2 `getSpellDamage` via `spell_damage_range`). `COMBAT_FORMULA_LEVELMAGIC` uses `get_spell_coeff()` too. `functions.lua` keeps a fallback that calls `getSpellCoeff` when present. Editing `spell.levelMult` / `magicMult` retunes the live spell pack. *(July 2026)*

231. **Defend Following + armor Tier-2 hook** (`crcombat.cc:245-247`, `GetArmorStrength`): `defend_fight_mode_for_target` must force DEFENSIVE when player `follow_target` is set (772 `Following`), not only when `attack_target` is None; monster chase `follow_target` is not Following. `armor_reduction` must call Tier-2 `getArmorReduction` when registered. *(July 2026)*

232. **Single production RNG: per-world glibc for both eras** (`crskill.cc:535`, `crcombat.cc:304`, `GlibcRngState`): `GameWorld::parity_rng` is the sole simulation stream (772 and 1098). `ParityRngSource` / `ai_rng` (`StdRng`) removed. Combat math takes `&GlibcRngState`; sim harness (`TFS_SIM_SEED`) still overrides via process `sim_*` when enabled. Free `parity_*` helpers remain for unit/sim fallbacks only — live paths use `world.parity_*` / `&self.parity_rng`. *(July 2026)*

233. **Monster armor/defend must mirror `GetArmorStrength` / `GetDefendValue`, not XML-only** (`crcombat.cc:191,286`): Strike snapshots used race `armor`/`defense` fields only. Decompile sums race armor + equipped `CLOTHES`/`ARMOR` at matching `BODYPOSITION`, and defend priority is shield → close → throw → missile(0) → `RaceData.Defend`. Fix: immutable `race_*` bases + `monster_get_armor_strength` / `monster_get_defend_value` / `monster_has_shield`; `melee_defense_snapshot_for` Monster arm uses them; `recompute_monster_combat_from_equipment` uses `race_*` so recompute is not double-additive. Spawn loot still bags weapons/shields (`crnonpl.cc:2071`) — body armor from `CreateAtCreature` is the common equipped case. *(July 2026)*

234. **KickCreature must run `NotifyGo` / `NotifyTurn` like `::Move`** (`operate.cc:1403–1434`, `cract.cc:1400–1581`, `monster_push.rs`): Relocate + `0x6D` alone left kicked monsters without dest-tile WAYPOINTS → `EarliestWalkTime`. They could step again ignoring floor speed (client looked desynced). Fix: shared `apply_notify_go_after_relocate` after kick broadcast; `apply_notify_turn` on kick only — walk steps already set facing in `internal_move_creature_step` (incl. chain turns). *(July 2026)*

235. **Spectator move `stackpos >= 10` must stay `0x6D`, not remove+appear** (`protocolgame.cpp:1830–1848`, OTC `allowAppearWalk`): TVP's `oldStackPos >= 10` spectator branch does remove+`sendAddCreature`, which never sets OTC `allowAppearWalk` → creature teleports (looks like a skip). KickCreature pushes often hit crowded/top-item tiles. Fix: same-viewport adjacent spectator moves always emit `0x6D` (with `0xFFFF+id` fallback when stack ∉ 0..9). Also announce-before-relocate + bump `next_wakeup` to `EarliestWalkTime` after kick so a pre-kick Go cannot dash mid-animation; real-772 stackpos adds `down_items` (GetObjectRNum) while OTC keeps TVP math. *(July 2026)*

236. **Poison arrow is periodic poison, not Earth HP** (`crcombat.cc:833-836`, `crmain.cc:582-593`): `DAMAGE_POISON_PERIODIC` refreshes `SetTimer(SKILL_POISON, Damage, 3, 3, -1)` when stronger than current timer — a DoT. Applying `CombatType::Earth` via `combat_execute` front-loads damage and skips poison immunity/FX. Keep TFS `ConditionDamage` domain; strength from `poisondamagecycles`. *(July 2026 — PvE re-audit)*

237. **Distance hit trains twice when learning is open** (`crcombat.cc:793-803`, `crskill.cc:535-552`): `Probe(..., Increase)` then `GetAttackDamage` → `ProbeValue(..., Increase)` = two `Increase(1)` + two `LearningPoints--`. A single probe-side `skill_increase` under-trains distance and burns LP slower than 772. Melee only needs one (CloseAttack has no separate Probe). *(July 2026 — PvE re-audit)*

238. **Poison arrow is Lua weapons domain, not native Earth** (`poison_arrow.lua`, `weapons.cpp` executeUseWeapon): Same seam as burst — `onUseWeapon` owns physical (`COMBAT_FORMULA_SKILL`) + poison DoT via `Condition(CONDITION_PARAM_CYCLE)` (772 `SetTimer` shape / `poisondamagecycles`). Do not treat unscripted `CombatType::Earth` as the intended path; that is fallback only when `has_on_use` is false. *(July 2026)*

239. **Distance hit trains twice** (`crcombat.cc:793-803`): Probe Increase then GetAttackDamage ProbeValue Increase — even when Lua `onUseWeapon` owns damage. Burn LP on both; ActivateLearning may refresh LP=30 after DamageDone. *(July 2026)*

240. **772 level/vocation gates are wand/rod Lua, not melee weapons** (`crcombat.cc:62-76`, `wands.lua`/`rods.lua`): Gate via `WandDef.level` + vocation name keys. TFS `weapon:vocation(name, bool)` bool is `showInDescription`, not allow/deny — any listed name is allowed. Ordinary swords/clubs have no 772 level req on this path. *(July 2026)*

241. **Hand/ammo equip → DelayAttack(2000)** (`crcombat.cc:128-147` CheckCombatValues): Mirror weapon identity change on Left/Right/Ammo postAdd/postRemove Owner link. *(July 2026)*

242. **Delayed StopAttack uses RoundNr, not server_ms** (`crcombat.cc:513-553`, `crmain.cc:414`): `StopAttack(Delay)` with Delay≠0 sets `LatestAttackTime = RoundNr + Delay` without clearing AttackDest. `Attack()` expires when `LatestAttackTime < RoundNr` (strict `<`, silent — no TARGETLOST). `SetAttackDest !Follow` clears the flag (`:438`) — do not confuse with `EarliestAttackTime`. Primary caller: `StartLogout(..., StopFight=false)` → delay 60 (`connections.cc:38` dead conn). *(July 2026)*

243. **Dead-connection map presence** (`connections.cc:37-38`, `crmain.cc:404-426`, `:1113-1124`): `Logout(0, false)` clears the connection then `StartLogout(false, false)` — body stays until `LogoutPossible` (combat lock / no-logout field). Idle kick uses `StopFight=true` (clears attack immediately); command-timeout / TCP shed uses `false` (`StopAttack(60)`). `BlockLogout` must not extend `EarliestLogoutRound` when `Connection == NULL` (`crmain.cc:444-448`). *(July 2026)*

244. **Relog TakeOver keeps the live body** (`connections.cc:231-252`, `crplayer.cc:721-775`): If `player_by_guid` exists — reject when dead or `LoggingOut && LogoutPossible==0`; else detach old TCP **without** `StartLogout` (CharacterID already 0), clear `LoggingOut`/`LogoutAllowed`, `StopAttack(0)`, leave channels, close containers, re-send login packets. Do **not** spawn a second creature from the DB load. *(July 2026)*

245. **Monster CASTING physical AllowDefense + armor** (`magic.cc:147-151`, `crmain.cc:624`): CASTING builds `TDamageImpact(..., AllowDefense=true)`. Rust must run the same defend+armor path as Lua AoE `BLOCKSHIELD`/`BLOCKARMOR` before HP apply — shared `mitigate_physical_spell_damage`. Non-physical elements skip both. *(July 2026 — monster combat audit)*

246. **Speed MDAct is percent of Go Act, not raw XML percent** (`magic.cc:226-250`): `flat_delta = (base_speed × percent) / 100`; if `percent < -100` → `-Act - 20`. Duration ms→ProcessSkills rounds. Haste (percent≥0) is non-aggressive so Target=0 defenses still cast. *(July 2026 — monster combat audit)*

247. **Drunk Power = drunkness/20 clamp 6** (`magic.cc:260-286`): XML keeps raw `drunkness` (e.g. 120); store `drunkenness` Power 0..=6 + Drunk condition timer. Clear Power on timer end. *(July 2026 — monster combat audit)*

248. **CASTING Target is `follow_target`, not AttackDest** (`crnonpl.cc:2572`, `monster_targets.rs`): Rust `follow_target` = C++ `Target`; `attack_target` = `Combat.AttackDest` (melee SetAttackDest only when Fist>0). Aim spells at `follow_target` only. Fist-only Attack: never synthesize DistanceAttack range 3 for spellcasters. *(July 2026 — monster combat audit)*

249. **Invisible stays CONDITION_INVISIBLE** (assassin.mon Outfit(0) parity): TFS XML `name="invisible"` → `ConditionType::Invisible`, not empty Outfit. Outfit illusions use `ConditionOutfit` (`monster=` / `item=`). *(July 2026 — monster combat audit)*

250. **772 NPC parity is single-focus queue semantics, not keyword-tree topics** (`crnonpl.cc` `TBehaviourDatabase::react`, `TNPC::TalkStimulus` / `IdleStimulus`; `operate.cc` `Talk`): Normal player SAY stimulates same-floor NPCs in a 3×3 range. Each NPC owns one interlocutor plus shared Topic/Price/Amount/Type/Data, handles other players through BUSY + deduplicated FIFO Queue, and times out after 30 one-second rounds. Matching chooses the greatest condition count with declaration-order ties; `!` short-circuits, `%1/%2` cap at 500, and replies use 1000 ms first delay plus `3100 + (byte_len/2)*100` ms. Actions are immediate, ordered, and non-transactional except speech/state/walk scheduling through ToDo. Keep canonical NPC authoring in TFS-style Lua; import legacy files offline rather than adding a runtime `.npc`/`.ndb` engine. *(July 2026 — NPC system audit)*

251. **NPC-0 corpus: four non-utf8 files + `String`/`Bless`/`Town`/`Promote` are not 772 parser vocabulary** (`tasks/npc-corpus-inventory.json`): Inventory of all 337 `.npc` + 39 `.ndb` found `brodrosch`/`captain bluebear`/`captain seahorse`/`uzon` as non-utf8 (latin-1 decode). `String=` (spell-name session var + `TeachSpell(String)`), `Bless(…)`, `Town(…)`, and bare `Promote` appear in `data/npc/behavior` but are absent from `crnonpl.cc` action/expression tables — importer (NPC-2) must reject or explicitly extend, never silently drop. Identifiers are lowercased at parse (`script.cc` `getIdentifier`); `Questvalue`/`SetQuestvalue` case variants are fine. *(July 2026 — NPC-0)*

252. **Spell fail messages are `TALK_FAILURE_MESSAGE`, not status/console** (`sending.cc:351-352` `SendMessage(TALK_FAILURE_MESSAGE)`; TFS `sendCancelMessage` → `MESSAGE_STATUS_SMALL`): `player_say_spell` was calling `send_player_status_message` (`TALK_STATUS_MESSAGE` = 21), which OTC puts in the chat/console. Use `send_cancel_message` / `failure_message_type` (772: 23). Wording: "You do not have enough mana." / "You must learn this spell first." (`sending.cc:323-326`). GM/god: `IgnoreSpellCheck` + `HasInfiniteMana` (772 `ALL_SPELLS` + `UNLIMITED_MANA`); stock gamemaster group had `cannotusespells=true` — flipped so GMs can cast free. *(July 2026 — spell fail UX)*

253. **GM/god skip PZ lock via `NotGainInFight`** (`player.cpp:2246` `addInFightTicks`; 772 `NO_LOGOUT_BLOCK` at `crmain.cc:438`): Gate once in `player_block_logout` so spells, melee, and AoE all skip Infight / PZ / logout block. Groups already set `notgaininfight=true` for gamemaster/god — was unloaded until `PLAYER_FLAG_NOT_GAIN_IN_FIGHT` mapped. *(July 2026)*

254. **Spawn `forced = !startup` put monsters in walls** (`info.cc:911+` `SearchSpawnField`; `spawn_placement.rs` `probe_spawn_tile`): Respawn passed `forced=true`, and probe did `login_possible = forced || queryAdd…` — any tile with ground became placeable, including UNPASS+UNMOVE walls (rats/spiders stuck). 772 has no force path: object-loop UNPASS+UNMOVE rejects login. Fix: probe mirrors `SearchSpawnField` object flags (no forced short-circuit); TFS `forced` polarity is `startup` (IGNOREBLOCKITEM only, never override failed queryAdd). *(July 2026 — Thais z=8 wall spawns)*

255. **Gorn spiders leak into sewers via Bank+wp0 “walls”** (`info.cc:962` Unpass; lesson 171 cliff clear-solid): Spider home `32377,32208,z=8` (under Gorn `32377,32200,z=7`) uses extended `SearchSpawnField` distance 10 — Chebyshev-reaches rat sewers at `32367,32216`. Dirt walls/earth are objects.srv `Bank+Unpass+wp0`; OTB clears `blockSolid` so players walk cliffs, so `is_unpassable()` was false and BFS `expansion_ok` flooded through the wall into the sewer corridor at y≈32214. Fix: `is_unpassable_for_field` (Bank+`waypoints_raw==0`) for spawn BFS. XML radii match `monster.db` — not a spawn-file bug. *(July 2026; walk gap → lesson 256)*

256. **Bank+wp0 spawn Unpass left monsters walking dirt walls** (lesson 171/255): Spawn probe used field Unpass, but `monster_move_possible_planning` / `tile_query_add_monster` still trusted cleared `blockSolid` — rats/spiders stepped through the same cliffs. Fix: `ItemType::is_unpassable_for_field` (Bank+wp0) shared by spawn + monster MovePossible + FillMap; monster/NPC `queryAdd` rejects cleared zero-waypoint banks only (players unchanged). *(July 2026)*

257. **Players pathfind through dirt walls to ladders** (lesson 171 clear-solid too broad): `build_bank_unpass_clear_solid` cleared `blockSolid` on **all** Bank+Unpass+wp0 (dirt wall, earth, water, mountain). OTBM only needs srv `"a mountain"` walkable as cliff rock-soil. Fix: solidify every `Unpass` (incl. Bank walls); clear solid **only** for name `"a mountain"`. Re-run `patch-otb-waypoints --flags-only`. Monsters still use `is_unpassable_for_field` on mountains. *(July 2026)*

258. **Spell runes: `Item:getId()` must return server type, and look needs register-time ItemType patch** (`luascript.cpp:6399` `luaItemGetId`; `15889–15895` register; `item.cpp:951–1003` look): Rust `ItemRef::getId` returned the SlotMap instance key, so `Player:conjureItem` (`leftItem:getId() == 2260`) never matched a blank rune — also broke door/lever scripts. Fix: resolve `item_type` via `ScriptContext`. Look showed bare `"spell rune"` because (1) `item_look` skipped the rune arm and (2) `rune:runeMagicLevel` wrote a separate `rune_magic_level` field instead of C++ `setMagicLevel`, so ItemType never got `runeMagLevel`/`charges`. Patch ItemType on spell load (`apply_rune_spell_defs`); parse `runespellname`; implement rune look suffix. Blank runes must still be in left/right hand (`conjureFromHandsOnly`). *(July 2026 — SD rune look + conjure)*

259. **Signs/blackboards need OTBM `ATTR_TEXT` + `allowDistRead` look** (`iomap.cpp` `OTBM_ITEM` → `unserializeItemNode`; `item.cpp:1422–1449`): Map load only took the `u16` item id from `ItemNodeProps` and discarded the attr blob, so sign text never reached `ItemAttributes`. Look also always appended `.` and skipped the `You read:` / far-away / empty branches. Fix: `parse_item_blob` on OTBM props after the id; port `allowDistRead` suffix (distance ≤ 4). *(July 2026)*

260. **forgotten.otbm Remere attrs 23–28 ≠ TFS `AttrTypes_t`** (RME `iomap_otbm.h` `OTBM_ATTR_KEYNUMBER`…`CHESTQUESTNUMBER`): Bytes 23–28 are u16 key/door fields on the map, but stock TFS `ATTR_CONTAINER_ITEMS`/`ATTR_NAME`/`ATTR_ARTICLE`/`ATTR_WEIGHT` expect u32/string — EOF / Invalid UTF-8 spam, attrs dropped. Map path uses `parse_otbm_item_blob` (custom attrs `keynumber`…); DB path keeps TFS numbering. Prop strings: Latin-1 like C++ `std::string`, not strict UTF-8. *(July 2026)*

261. **Nested `with_lua_mutation_scope` must restore parent** (`lua_mutation.rs`): `item:remove` during `conjureItem` fires inventory equip hooks that enter a new mutation scope; old impl cleared `MUTATION_WORLD` to null on inner exit → `addItem` failed with "Lua mutation scope not active". Save/restore previous world + result cells. *(July 2026)*

262. **`UseWithCreature` was parsed but never handled** (`game_loop.rs`): needTarget runes (SD etc.) send `USE_WITH_CREATURE`; packet fell through catch-all → silent no-op. Wire to ToDo use-with; `validate_use_ex_target_ref` accepts creatures on the target tile (obj2 is not always an item). *(July 2026)*

263. **`VARIANT_NUMBER` is SlotMap `u64`, not wire `u32`** (`combat.rs` `resolve_variant_center`): Rune `onCastSpell` sets `variant.number` from `CreatureId::as_ffi()` (packed key). Reading as `u32` failed conversion → misleading "missing number field". Read as `u64`; `get_player_position` already resolves any creature. *(July 2026)*

264. **Rune far-use is `allowFarUse`, not only OTB DistUse** (`actions.cpp` `canExecuteAction`/`canUseFar`; `idle_stimulus` Use Obj2 arm): Inventory SD/GFB walked to the target because ToDo only treated DistUse as shoot-from-here; Spell Lua `rune:allowFarUse(true)` was ignored. Treat registered runes with `allow_far_use` like far-use (TFS `areInRange<7,5>`, no walk). needTarget miss: cancel + `CONST_ME_POFF` (`spells.cpp:743-746`). Floor AoE runes use position variant when `need_target` is false. *(July 2026)*

265. **772 throw/move P0 parity** (`cract.cc` `TCreature::Move`; `operate.cc` `CheckMapDestination`/`IsMapBlocked`; `info.cc` `GetTopObject`/`ThrowPossible`): The item-throw path still used TFS 1.4.2 `canThrowObjectTo` (Bresenham + per-item `block_projectile`) and a hard 15-tile pickupable cap. Fix: wire `Map::throw_possible(..., power=1)` for 772 major-axis LOS, remove the pickupable range limit (non-takeable keep `ObjectInRange(2)`), and replace the destination predicate with `IsMapBlocked` (`BANK`/`UNPASS`/`UNLAY`/`HANG` hooks). `UNLAY` has no OTB bit, so derive it from the XML `unlay` attribute at runtime. `GetTopObject(true)` is implemented as `get_top_object_for_move` using `always_on_top()` and `is_cip_priority_bottom()` to skip TOP/BOTTOM/CLIP render classes. *(July 2026)*

266. **PZ-entry lock is walk `queryAdd`, not combat-only** (`crplayer.cc:366-369` `ENTERPROTECTIONZONE`; `crmain.cc:439-443` `BlockLogout`): `earliest_protection_zone_round` was stored but never enforced. Gate in `tile_query_add_player`: locked + dest PZ + not already in PZ → `PlayerIsPzLocked` (TFS enter-PZ message). Same 772 snapback set as combat’s `ActionNotPermittedInProtectionZone`. `IgnoreProtectionZone` skips. *(August 2026)*

267. **NoLogout is a tile flag, never `ZoneType::NoLogout`** (`iomap.cpp` OTBM→`TILESTATE_NOLOGOUT`; TFS `getZone()` never returns `ZONE_NOLOGOUT`; 772 `IsNoLogoutField`): Logout checked `zone == NoLogout`, but `convert_otbm_flags` only ORs the flag. Fix: `flags & NOLOGOUT`. Do not auto-flag house tiles as PZ under 772 — houses and PZ are orthogonal (`crnonpl.cc` `IsProtectionZone || IsHouse`). *(August 2026)*

268. **UseItemEx rune obj2 is position-based; empty ground is not an `ItemId`** (`creature_todo.rs` `validate_use_ex_target_ref`; `tile.rs` `item_id_for_use`): Ground is `Option<u16>` type id only. Requiring a resolvable item or creature rejected floor aims → enqueue `NotPossible` with no POFF, so SD miss never cast and GFB never fired. TFS `playerUseItemEx` validates from-item only; `RuneSpell` uses `toPosition`. Accept any loaded map tile by position; miss vs AoE stays in `player_cast_rune`. *(August 2026)*

269. **Fluid vial look omitted ` of manafluid`** (`item_look.rs`; `item.cpp` ~1407–1421 / TVP `item.cpp` ~1026–1031): `item_get_description_cpp` had no fluid-container/splash arm, so look was only `"a vial."`. Also OTBM/DB wrote fluid subtype into `count` without `set_fluid_type`, and `get_sub_type` read only the attr. Fix: append ` of {items[subType].name}` / `. It is empty`; fall back to `count` when fluid attr unset; sync `set_fluid_type` on map/DB load (allow count 0 for empty). *(August 2026)*

270. **UseItem on bare ground needs no SlotMap `ItemId`** (`creature_todo.rs` `validate_use_object_ref`; `container_ui.rs` `resolve_ground_use_type` / `player_use_ground_core`): Ground is `body.ground: Option<u16>` only — `resolve_use_object` / `item_id_for_use` return `None` when the item list is empty. Enqueue failed with `NotPossible` so clients never walked to rope holes / floor teleports / dirt. Accept ground via **TypeID match only** (772 `GetObject` `info.cc:412–419` — not empty-list / stackpos-0 shortcuts); execute teleport types through the floor-change path, else `CannotUseThisObject` (`NOTUSABLE` / "You cannot use this object."). Turn stays item-only. *(August 2026)*

271. **Spell exhaust was a no-op when Lua omitted `:cooldown()`** (`game_world_chat.rs` `player_say_spell`; `PendingSpell`; `magic.cc` `CheckMana` Delay): bare `delay_spell_ms(..., 0)` set `EarliestSpellTime = now`. Fix: `spell_exhaust_delay_ms` (explicit `:cooldown` or world Delay: open/NoPvp **2000**, OE **1000**); `PendingSpell` leaves `cooldown = 0` (unset) so that fallback applies — not TFS’s hard 1000 default, which under-exhausted MassCombat (UE) on open PvP. Strikes that need 1000 set `:cooldown(1000)`. Exhaust **check** outside `IgnoreSpellCheck`; only `HasNoExhaustion` skips. *(August 2026)*

272. **Exhaust = 772 `Earliest*Time` clocks; TFS Lua only tunes them** (`player_apply_spell_exhaust_ms` / `player_apply_rune_exhaust`; `cract.cc:765` multiuse; `magic.cc` CheckMana): Instant spells gate + set `EarliestSpellTime` (`:cooldown` or world Delay). `:group`/`:groupCooldown` write `spell_group_cooldown_end` (additive). Runes always set `EarliestMultiuseTime` (+1000); `cooldownSpellTime(true)` also bumps spell clock — **default false** so SD/GFB match 772 (rune does not block spoken spells). Not ConditionExhaust. *(August 2026)*

273. **Spell cast deducted mana/soul without `0xA0`** (`game_world_chat.rs` `player_say_spell`; `crskill.cc` `TSkillMana::Set` / `TSkillSoulpoints::Set`): Server mana changed but client bar stayed stale because `notify_magic_tries_gained` / `send_player_stats` only ran on magic-level advance. Always notify after CheckMana deduct (same as wand path). Related: Lua heal used `damage_done` only so heals skipped `notify_creature_healed`; condition DoT skipped combat notify — both now refresh stats/health bar. *(August 2026)*

274. **Support buffs vanished on relog** (`condition_blob.rs`; `login.rs` / `game_world_save.rs`; `login_out.rs`): Magic Shield / Haste / Invisibility lived only in RAM — `players.conditions` was never written and login forced `active_conditions: []`. 772 keeps them as skill Cycle fields (`crplayer.cc` Save/Load); TFS domain uses the PropStream blob (`Condition::serialize` / `isPersistent`). Fix: serialize persistent conditions on save, deserialize on load, `reapply_persisted_condition_effects` + real `send_player_icons` after login (was hardcoding icons `0`). *(August 2026)*

275. **Aggressive spells/runes PZ-locked vs monsters** (`game_world_chat.rs` `player_say_spell`; `container_ui.rs` rune cast): Cast path used `BlockLogout(..., true)`, so energy strike / UE on monsters set `EarliestProtectionZoneRound`. 772 `CastSpell` / `UseMagicItem` use `BlockLogout(60, false)` (`magic.cc:3636-3638, 4304-4306`); PZ lock only when damage hits a player (`Target->Type == PLAYER`) or fire/poison/energy fields. *(August 2026)*

276. **Firebomb / field runes: no damage + delayed 772 client crash on monster interaction** (`otb.rs` `is_cip_priority_bottom`; `magic_field.rs`; `game_world_item_cylinder.rs`): (1) Crash: MagicField wrongly classified as Cip `PRIORITY_BOTTOM`. 772 `objects.srv` fire/poison/energy fields have **no** `Bottom` → `PRIORITY_LOW` after creatures (`map.cc:1999-2013`). Stock client inserts `0x6A` by .dat priority (LOW); server counted the field before the creature → next MoveCreature/update stackpos desync (`bug0000017`). Fix: BOTTOM = splash/pool only. (2) No damage: field runes use `CREATEITEM` with `(0,0)` damage; real hurt is `MagicField::onStepInField` / `AddItemField`. `movements.xml` `function="onStepInField"` is a **C++ native**, not Lua — registry load failed and `internal_add` called `on_step_in(None)` (no-op). Fix: native apply on place + walk land (initdamage + DoT condition from items.xml `field.*`). *(August 2026)*

277. **Hole/stairs down z=7→8 crashed stock 772 client with `rz=-1` / bug0000013** (`map_description.rs` `send_notify_go`; crash log `Map.cpp:378`): NotifyGo led with `0x6D` (old→new including z=8) then `0xBF` FloorDown. Client FloorDown increments z from the already-updated centre and reads the wrong floor set/offsets → assert `In(rz,0,MAPSIZE_Z-1)`. TVP/`send_move_creature_player` already used `0x6C` remove (no new z) for surface→underground. Fix: same `0x6C` for `orig.z==7 && dest.z>=8` in `send_notify_go`; keep `0x6D` for other adjacent moves. Repro packet: `(32380,32205,7)→(32380,32204,8)`. *(August 2026)*

278. **Door `item:transform` must reset+apply tile flags** (`decay_apply.rs` `change_item_type`; `map/mod.rs` `reset_item_tile_flags` / `apply_item_tile_flags`; TFS `Tile::updateThing` `tile.cpp:963-966`): In-place type change that only swapped `item_type` left `BLOCKSOLID` sticky after closed→open, so walk still blocked. Also: Lua `TILESTATE_*` had been shifted by a phantom `TILESTATE_REFRESH` (BLOCKSOLID was `1<<18` instead of `1<<17`); fix globals to match `tile.h` / core `flags`. `item:getPosition()` must return `Position` userdata (not an `(x,y,z)` tuple) so `doors.lua` `item:getPosition() + Position(-1,0,0)` works — interpret RHS coords as signed i16. *(August 2026 — doors Phase 2)*

279. **`item:remove` "item has no parent"** (`food.lua` after Action onUse): Loot/corpse paths (`roll_loot_block_glibc`, `move_body_item_into_corpse`, spawn bag fill) called `Container::add_item` without stamping `Item.parent`. Use from an open corpse/bag then hit `lua_script_item_remove` → error. Fix: stamp `Cylinder::Container` at those hubs; `resolve_or_repair_item_parent` discovers from registry/slots/map when parent is stale. *(August 2026)*

280. **772 blank name/HP bar + crash = known-creature limit 1300 vs client table 150** (`creature_known.rs`; TVP `protocolgame.cpp:652`; CipSoft `connections.hh` `KnownCreatureTable[150]`): Server never emitted `remove_known` until 1301 entries, so after ~150 unique sightings (roam, chase, hole-down) the stock client table overflowed. Later `0x62` known appears missed the client slot → no name/bar → desync crash. Fix: `ProtocolCaps::known_creature_limit` 150 (772) / 1300 (1098); pass into `check_creature_known`. *(August 2026)*

281. **Door keys need Remere custom-attr string aliases, not TVP bitflags** (`doors.lua` Phase 3; `item_blob` OTBM 23–28; TVP `enums.h` `ITEM_ATTRIBUTE_KEYNUMBER=1<<25`): Map load already stores `keynumber` / `keyholenumber` as custom attrs (TFS `ATTR_NAME`/`WEIGHT` collide at those OTBM bytes). TVP int bitflags also collide with TFS 1.4.2 `STORE_ITEM`+. Lua constants are the string keys; `getAttribute`/`hasAttribute`/`setAttribute` accept number (TFS bitflags) or string (Remere). Also: `Item:isItem` / `Tile:getTopVisibleThing` for the key use-with re-resolve path. *(August 2026 — doors Phase 3)*

282. **Quest/level doors need player storage, not new door code** (`doors.lua` Phase 4; `player.cpp` `getStorageValue`/`addStorageValue`): Remere `doorquestnumber`/`doorquestvalue`/`doorlevel` attrs were already wired in Phase 3. Missing piece was `player:getStorageValue` / `setStorageValue` over `PlayerPersistBaseline.storage` (same map NPC quest values use). Missing key → `-1`; `set(-1)` erases; Lua rejects `PSTRG_RESERVED_RANGE`. Legacy actionid movement scripts stay for Phase 6 MoveEvents — `doors.lua` stays Remere-attr authoritative. *(August 2026 — doors Phase 4)*

283. **House doors: native `Door::canUse` before Lua, not house-guest invite** (`house.rs` / `container_ui.rs`; `house.cpp` ~535; `actions.cpp` ~304): House **guest** list does **not** open doors — only owner/subowner/`CanEditHouses` or the **per-door** access list (`house_lists.listid` = door id). Deny returns `NOTPOSSIBLE` → "Sorry, not possible." (same as TFS/TVP cancel), not the quest sealed string. `ATTR_HOUSEDOORID` must call `set_door_id` (was read-and-discard). Guild `@name` lines deferred. *(August 2026 — doors Phase 5)*

284. **Quest/level door auto-close is creature-walk MoveEvent, not item cylinder** (`closing_doors.lua` Phase 6; `movement.cpp` `onCreatureMove`; `tile.cpp` postRemove/postAdd): XML equip registry alone never fired StepOut. Need `MoveEvent()` revscripts + load `scripts/movements/**` after door-table inject, and `move_creature_on_map` must call StepOut/StepIn **after** unregister/register so `getCreatureCount()` is 0 when the last walker leaves (otherwise door never closes). `executeStep` needs `fromPosition` (4th arg) for level-door kick. `getItemByGroup(MAGICFIELD)` matches **type tag** (OTB group deprecated). `item:getType()` must return `ItemTypeRef` for `:isMovable()`. *(August 2026 — doors Phase 6)*

285. **Quest door blink + no auto-close = ignored `pushMovement` + StepOut without Lua scope** (`spawn_lifecycle.rs` / `walk/mod.rs` / `lua_scope.rs`; `luascript.cpp` `luaCreatureTeleportTo`; `doors.lua` / `closing_doors.lua`): (1) `teleportTo(pos, true)` ignored `push_movement` and always used teleport remove+mapdesc — door entry blinked. Fix: `internal_teleport_player(..., push_movement)` uses walk packets when adjacent (`Map::moveCreature` `forceTeleport = !pushMove`). (2) `move_creature_on_map` fired StepOut/In without `with_lua_mutation_scope` / `with_lua_context`, so `Tile()` / `item:transform` failed on walk-off (use-path nested under `fire_on_use_action` so open still worked). Fix: `fire_creature_step_events`. *(August 2026)*

286. **Quest door StepOut before move packets crashes stock 772** (`walk/mod.rs` `flush_pending_creature_step_events`; `map.cpp` ~309–327): After fixing StepOut scope, `closing_doors.lua` `item:transform` emitted `0x6B` **before** NotifyGo/move while the client still had the creature on the door tile → debug assert. C++ order is relocate → **sendCreatureMove** → postRemove StepOut. Defer step events in `pending_creature_step_events` and flush after packets (`on_walk`, `internal_teleport_player`, kick/throw paths). *(August 2026)*

287. **Burning DoT was Event-every-ProcessSkills, not 772 MaxCount** (`process_skills.rs` / `condition.rs` `skill_count`/`skill_max_count`; `crskill.cc:179-196`, `crmain.cc:600`): Profile `{ dmg=10, ticks=8 }` was treated as “8 one-second damage ticks.” Decompile `TSkill::Process` only calls `Event` when `Count <= 0`, then `Count = MaxCount` and `Cycle` steps toward 0 — fire `SetTimer(..., 8, 8)` ⇒ ~8s between 10-dmg Events; `Cycle = Damage/10` (or items.xml `field.cycles`) is Events remaining. Wire `skill_count`/`skill_max_count` on apply (fields, Lua) and countdown in `process_skills`. *(August 2026)*

288. **Moving tables left ghost `BLOCKSOLID` on the source tile** (`game_world_item_move.rs` / `detach_item_from_tile`; `tile.cpp` `removeThing`→`resetTileFlags`): Tile→Tile (and Tile→container/inventory) full moves called `remove_item_by_id` without resetting flags. Destination got `apply_item_tile_flags`, source kept `BLOCKSOLID` → unwalkable empty tile. `internal_remove_item_from_tile` already reset; move paths now share `detach_item_from_tile`. *(August 2026)*

289. **Door-close shove is 772 `ClearField`, not ad-hoc Lua offsets** (`clear_field.rs` / `Game.clearField`; `moveuse.cc:569-617`): Click-close used E/S/W/N `!BLOCKSOLID` only — missing `BANK`, `JumpPossible` fallback, and `MoveAllObjects` (creatures+movables; delete pool/field). Native `clear_field` mirrors decompile; `doors.lua` / `closing_doors.lua` call it before transform. Magic-field remove no longer targets the shove dest by mistake. *(August 2026)*

290. **772 player death left ghost model + OK no-op** (`spawn_lifecycle.rs` / `game_world_lifecycle.rs` / `game_loop.rs`; `crplayer.cc:324-337` `TPlayer::Death`, `connections.cc:269` `Die`, `receiving.cc:17-21` `CommandAllowed`, TVP `game.cpp:545` `removeCreature`): (1) `on_creature_removed_for_spawn` skipped disappear for players, and `broadcast_creature_disappear` excluded self — corpse appeared but the tile creature stayed (spectators + OTClient local player). (2) Death removed the body then left `conn_to_creature` stale; OK→`0x14` hit `player_logout_allowed` → `false` (no creature / Infight) so TCP never closed. Fix: always broadcast remove including self; send `"You are dead.\n"` + `dead_connections` (`CONNECTION_DEAD`); gate non-logout packets; OK disconnects dead sessions; save on death before teardown; clear ConnId mapping after remove. *(August 2026)*

291. **Change outfit: 772 SET_OUTFIT has no addons/mount** (`game_parse.rs` / `game_world_outfit.rs`; TVP `protocolgame.cpp:790` / `sendOutfitWindow`): Parsing 1098's trailing addons+mount on 772 desyncs the stream. Window layout also diverges — stock client gets first/last looktype range; OTClient gets named list without addons; 1098 gets list+addons+mounts. `canWear` uses sex-matched `outfits.xml` + premium/unlocked/owned; owned entries hydrate from reserved storage `PSTRG_OUTFITS_RANGE`. Apply updates `base.outfit` and broadcasts codec `0x8E` unless `CONDITION_OUTFIT` (illusion) or invisible. Mounts still deferred. *(August 2026)*

292. **Death save must temple + full vitals, not death tile / HP=1** (`game_world_lifecycle.rs` `prepare_player_death_save`; TFS/TVP `Player::death`): Saving on death wrote `pos` = corpse tile and clamped `health` to 1. Relog placed the player back in the dungeon; monsters (or persisted DoT) killed them instantly in a loop. Fix: restore HP/mana, clear conditions, and write **temple** into the save row as TFS `loginPosition` — do **not** move live `base.position` before remove (that desynced `0x6C` / map unregister and crashed clients). *(August 2026)*

293. **Level up/down set capacity to ~0 — oz vs centi-oz unit mismatch** (`creature/vocation.rs` `VocationProfile::from_def` / `recalculate_vitals`; TFS `vocation.cpp` `gainCap = xml * 100`, `iologindata.cpp` `cap * 100`, `player.cpp` `capacity += getCapGain()`): Login/save already use centi-oz on `Player.capacity`, but level change wrote `base_cap + gain_cap * (level-1)` using **oz** from `vocations.lua` (400 + 25×n). Free capacity = capacity − inventory_weight then underflows to 0 (wire also `/100`). Fix: convert `base_cap`/`gain_cap` to centi-oz when building `VocationProfile` from content defs; keep `VocationDef` in oz for data-pack parity. *(August 2026)*

294. **PvP P0 numbers — half damage + OE kill XP** (`idle_stimulus.rs` / `death.rs` / `combat/math.rs`; `crmain.cc:497–501`, `crplayer.cc:339–340`, `crcombat.cc:922–934`): (1) Player↔player non-DoT applies `(d+1)/2` before absorb; DoT ticks use `attacker=None` so they skip (= `*_PERIODIC`). (2) Dead `pvp_exp_cap` was an identity “cap”; replaced with `pvp_kill_experience_amount` MaxLevel scale (`(L*11)/10`). Player-kill pool is `Exp/20` only on `WorldType::PvpEnforced` (0 on open PvP); same `party_id` skips the share. Lua `pvpExpCap` remains the MaxLevel ratio, not a pool ceiling. *(August 2026)*

295. **PvP P1 skulls — AttackedPlayers / Aggressor / observer marks** (`player/combat/skulls.rs`; `crplayer.cc:1414–1676`, `crmain.cc:1102`): Session lists + `Aggressor` + `Former*` (+5-round justify window). `RecordAttack` on open PvP only (SetAttackDest, Attack, Damage responsible). `IsAttackJustified` unlocks secure retaliation. `GetPlayerkillingMark` is observer-relative (Red→White→Green→Yellow); AddCreature + `0x90` use it, not `base.skull`. `PlayerkillerEnd` loads from `skulltime` (login expiry); murder assignment is P2. Party = live `party_id` only (no FormerParty yet). *(August 2026)*

296. **PvP P2 murder / red / secure AoE** (`skulls.rs` / `config.rs` / `aoe.rs`; `crplayer.cc:1492–1584`, `crmain.cc:822–826`, `magic.cc:708–728`): `MurderTimestamps[20]` ring + multi-window `CheckPlayerkilling` (day **86000**/3→red/6→ban, week/month 5·10 / 10·20). Unjust kill → admin warning, `PlayerkillerEnd = Now + redSkullDuration`, ban ladder → `account_bans` + kick. Player death → `BlockLogout(whiteSkullTime)` on Attacker+Responsible and `RecordMurder` for Responsible + MostDangerous. Persist CSV `murder_timestamps`; Lua `getMurderTimestamps` / `getPlayerKillerEnd`. `CheckAffectedPlayers` blocks secure AoE on unmarked players. FormerParty (+5 rounds) in justify + PvP exp skip; green stays live-only. Ignore `killsToRedSkull` / black / frag decay for 772. *(August 2026)*

297. **772 player combat P0** (`combat/math.rs`, `player/combat/{values,strike,ranged}.rs`, `player/inventory/util.rs`, `creature/player.rs`, `formulas.rs`; `crcombat.cc` / `crskill.cc`): (1) `probe_hit` draws chance `rand()` only after skill gate — unconditional second draw desynced glibc. (2) `CombatWeapons` snapshot + `min_req_level`/`voc_equip_names` gates + Fist; categorical `player_weapon_distance` vs in-strike `player_weapon_max_range`; CheckCombatValues diffs cached snapshot. (3) `dact_skills`/`mdact_skills` + Min floor (`skill_level`); `skill_act` for Probe. (4) ProbeValue `Increase` before Get; `SkillTryScaling::Flat` (772) vs `Rated` (1098). Wands stay on Lua `WandDef`. *(August 2026)*

298. **772 player combat P1** (`enums.rs`, `idle_stimulus.rs`, `condition.rs`, `process_skills.rs`, `creature/base.rs` CombatList, `death.rs`, `creature_todo.rs`, `notifications.rs`; `crmain.cc` / `crcombat.cc` / `cract.cc` / `crskill.cc`): (1) `PoisonPeriodic`/`FirePeriodic`/`EnergyPeriodic` arm before HP — poison strength-gated, fire/energy unconditional; `*DamageOrigin` on base; Event ticks use **instant** types + origin (PvP half applies on ticks, not arming). (2) `CombatList` ring (20 slots) + `exp_attribution_rounds` window for most-dangerous; summon `dmg/2` split. (3) Soul regen arm when exp ≥ level (`gain_soul_ticks`). (4) Invis re-check in CanToDoAttack + Attack. (5) Ranged `ToDoWait(100)` when distance ≠ 1. (6) `player_check_combat_values` on level Jump. *(August 2026)*

299. **772 player combat P2** (`idle_stimulus.rs`, `combat/mod.rs`, `game_world_lifecycle.rs`, `ranged.rs`, `otb.rs`/`items.rs`, `combat/math.rs`, `creature/player.rs`, `strike.rs`; codec `CreatureSquareWire`): (1) `SendMarkCreature` — 772 `0x86`+id+color vs 1098 `0x93`+id+`0x01`+color; fire on player damage taken. (2) ManaDrain early arm + MAGIC_RED/blue text; mana-shield **full** absorb names attacker (`crmain.cc:672-680`); partial absorb silent. (3) AoL only on `exact_lethal_blow` (`Damage == HitPoints`); full-slot clothes/`slot_position` scan; type 2173 = pack stand-in for `GetNewObjectType(77,12)`. (4) Ammo miss drop: BANK+!UNLAY+ThrowPossible. (5) Typed `AMMO*`/`THROW*` specials + native burst `disc_offsets(2)`/`EFFECT_FIRE_BURST=7` when unscripted (Lua still owns live pack). (6) `req_skill_tries` linear when mult&lt;1.05; level&gt;500 → -1; `remove_experience` abort when amount&gt;exp&amp;&amp;exp&gt;100k. (7) Vocation `melee_damage`/`dist_damage` only under `DamageFormula::Modern`. (8) Wand magic tries match `CheckMana` (`magic.cc` Increase). *(August 2026)*

300. **Shared `ExecuteCircleSpell` tile iteration** (`combat/aoe.rs` `collect_area_targets` / `filter_area_positions`, `idle_stimulus.rs`; `magic.cc:468-500`): Both the Lua `Combat:execute` path (`aoe.rs`) and the monster CASTING path (`idle_stimulus.rs`) had identical per-tile filtering loops — PZ skip, `ThrowPossible` LoS, `GetFirstObject` creature collection. Extracted `filter_area_positions(&tiles, los_origin, aggressive, skip_caster, skip_npcs)` → `AreaIteration { tiles, targets }`. Lua path calls `collect_area_targets(center, los_origin, &offsets, ...)` which converts offsets to positions then delegates. Monster path calls `filter_area_positions` directly with pre-computed tiles from `monster_idle_spell_tiles`. Callers retain effect broadcast, field creation, and impact application (different types per path). 772 inlines `ExecuteCircleSpell` in 7 places (`KillAllMonsters`, `MassCreateField`, `MassRaiseDead`, `MassHeal`, `CancelInvisibility`, `Challenge` + the canonical `ExecuteCircleSpell`); the remaining 5 will reuse `filter_area_positions` when ported. *(August 2026)*

301. **C2 poison field re-extension used wrong field** (`process_skills.rs`; `crskill.cc:1039,1070,1096`): All three DoT `Event` functions do `this->Cycle += 1` when standing on a matching AVOID field, but `Cycle` maps to different Rust fields per type. Poison `Cycle` = damage pool → `ConditionData::Damage::total_rank` (drains by `Range` per Event, `crskill.cc:983`). Fire/Energy `Cycle` = tick countdown → `ActiveCondition::timer_rounds_left` (decrements by 1 per Event, `crskill.cc:187`). The Rust C2 code always incremented `timer_rounds_left`, which is `None` for poison — a silent no-op that let poison expire even while standing on a poison field. Fix: dispatch on `cond.ctype` — poison extends `total_rank`, fire/energy extend `timer_rounds_left`. *(August 2026)*

302. **P-A — 772 Gate A pushability predicate** (`game_world_player_throw.rs`, `creature/monster.rs`; `operate.cc:439` `CheckMoveObject`, `crmain.cc:1356` `GetRaceUnpushable`, `crmain.cc:900` / `crnonpl.cc:2295` `IsPeaceful`): The player-push gate was using TFS `Monster::is_pushable()` (`pushable && speed != 0`) instead of the 772 race-flag predicate `GetRaceUnpushable(Race) && (WorldType != NON_PVP || !IsPeaceful())`. Three divergences fixed: (1) **P2** — 772 Gate A consults the race `unpushable` flag only, **not** `speed`; a `speed == 0` monster of a pushable race is pushable. Added `Monster::race_unpushable()` (= `!self.pushable`, the loaded race flag) distinct from `is_pushable()` (kept for TFS-style monster-AI kick gates). (2) **P2** — the NON_PVP peaceful exception was missing: in `NoPvp` worlds, an `unpushable`-race creature that is *peaceful* (player, NPC, or player-summon via `Master != 0 && IsCreaturePlayer(Master)`) **can** be pushed. Reused the existing `pub(crate) fn creature_is_peaceful` (`player/combat/mod.rs:744`) — no new helper file needed. (3) **P7** — NPC pushability was hardcoded `Npc(_) => false`; 772 has no `Race` for players/NPCs in this gate, so NPCs pass Gate A (`unpushable = false`) and are governed by Gate B/C `MovePossible` instead. **Observable message change (minor nit):** the blocked case now returns `ReturnValue::NotMoveable` ("You cannot move this object.") instead of `NotPossible` ("Sorry, not possible.") — correct per 772 `NOTMOVABLE`, but a client-visible change. **Data-pack caveat (V3):** the TFS data pack conflates 772's independent `unpushable` and `kickcreatures` race flags — `canPushCreatures="1"` forces `pushable=false` (`tfs-rust-content/src/monsters.rs:778-780`), so `race_unpushable() = !self.pushable` is correct for the existing pack, but a 772-accurate monster with `kickcreatures` but pushable cannot be expressed. Full audit: `docs/772_PLAYER_PUSH_AUDIT.md` (P-A is the first of phases P-A..P-E). *(August 2026)*

303. **P-B — `ToDoMove` creature-container delay (P1) + execute-time adjacency re-check (P12)** (`creature_todo.rs` `enqueue_player_move`, `idle_stimulus.rs` `execute_player_move`, `game_world_player_throw.rs` `player_push_creature`; `cract.cc:1123-1172` `ToDoMove`, `cract.cc:1144-1163` creature-container branch, `operate.cc:424` `ObjectAccessible`): Three issues fixed: (1) **P1 (pacing)** — `enqueue_player_move` always used `Wait{100}` for both items and creatures. 772 `ToDoMove` sets `Delay = 1000` + remaining walk cooldown (`EarliestWalkTime - ServerMilliseconds`) for creature containers (`cract.cc:1156-1159`), and `Delay = 100` for items (`cract.cc:1155`). The creature-container branch also requires the destination to have a `BANK` first object (ground tile) — `cract.cc:1145-1148`. Now `enqueue_player_move` resolves the source thing via `internal_get_thing_move` and branches: creature → `Wait{1000 + cooldown}` + BANK dest check; item → `Wait{100}`. (2) **Creature pushes were broken in the production packet flow** — `validate_move_object_ref` (called by both `enqueue_player_move` and `execute_player_move`) rejected `Thing::Creature` (only accepted `Thing::Item`), so creature pushes never reached `player_push_creature` through the ToDo engine. The P-A tests bypassed this by calling `player_push_creature` directly. Removed `validate_move_object_ref` from both call sites — `internal_get_thing_move` already does sprite validation for items, and `player_move_thing` re-resolves and handles both items and creatures. The dead `validate_move_object_ref` function was removed (Code Hygiene: "Remove Dead Code in the Same PR"). (3) **P12 (C7, execute-time adjacency)** — `CheckMoveObject` calls `ObjectAccessible(CreatureID, Obj, 1)` (`operate.cc:424`) at **execute** time, not just enqueue. The 1000ms wait lets the target walk away; 772 rejects at execute, Rust didn't. Added `object_in_range(actor, target_pos, 1)` at the top of `player_push_creature`, checking the creature's **current** position (not the enqueue-time `from_pos`). The check runs before Gate A, matching the 772 order (`ObjectAccessible` at `operate.cc:424` before the race-flag gate at `operate.cc:439`). **Test fixture fix:** P-A tests placed the actor 2 tiles away (only needed for `DelayAttack`); P12's adjacency check now requires the actor within range 1, so `setup_push_arena` and the NPC test were updated to place the actor adjacent (`dy=1`). *(August 2026)*
304. **P-C — `CheckMapDestination` creature-container arm (P3, P5, P6, P9, PZ)** (`game_world_player_throw.rs` `check_push_destination` / `tile_has_avoid`, `walk/walk_tile.rs` `tile_elevation_sum`, `tfs-rust-content/src/otb.rs` `ItemType::elevation`, `tfs-rust-content/src/items.rs` `apply_xml_attribute`; `operate.cc:493-532` `CheckMapDestination`, `info.cc:689` `GetHeight`, `enums.hh:760` `ELEVATION`): Five issues fixed: (1) **P3 (range cap)** — 772 limits creature pushes to 1 tile (`|Δx|>1 || |Δy|>1 || |Δz|>1 → OUTOFRANGE`); Rust had no cap. (2) **P5 (C1, elevation-sum gate)** — the existing height-24 check was dead code: it used `tile_has_height_n` (a TFS `Tile::hasHeight(n)` **count** of hasHeight items, true at `count >= 24` — never true), had no `dz` condition (nominally applied to same-floor pushes too), and `Elevation` was not parsed anywhere in `crates/`. 772 `GetHeight` (`info.cc:689`) is an **elevation sum** — it iterates the tile stack and sums the `ELEVATION` attribute (`enums.hh:760`) of every `HEIGHT`-flagged object. The gate is `sum < 24`. Added `pub elevation: i32` to `ItemType` (parsed from items.xml `<attribute key="elevation" value="N"/>`; default `0`), added `tile_elevation_sum(body, items_db, items) -> i32` mirroring `GetHeight`, and ported the up/down asymmetry: up-floor (`to.z == from.z - 1`) checks **origin** elevation sum → `NOROOM`; down-floor (`to.z == from.z + 1`) checks **dest** elevation sum → `NOWAY`; same-floor ignores elevation. (3) **P6 (dest AVOID)** — 772 rejects pushing another creature onto an `AVOID`-flagged tile (`CoordinateFlag(Dest, AVOID) → NOROOM`, `operate.cc:525`). `AVOID` maps to `MAGICFIELD` tile-state (`walk/walk_tile.rs:656`). Added `tile_has_avoid(pos)` helper. (4) **P9 (C6, ThrowPossible)** — `ThrowPossible(Orig, Dest, 1)` (`operate.cc:576`) is **not** trivially true at range 1: it tests `UNTHROW` on the dest tile and runs a floor-descent loop for `dz != 0`. Added `map.throw_possible(from, to, 1)` call → `CANNOTTHROW`. (5) **C5 (nesting)** — the `AVOID` and `PZ → non-PZ` checks are nested inside `if(OrigZ == DestZ || Type != MONSTER)` **after** `MovePossible` succeeds (`operate.cc:515-532`); the helper mirrors this with `if dz == 0 || !moving_is_monster`, with a placeholder for P-D's `MovePossible`. **Return value corrections:** the plan sketched `NotPossible` for OUTOFRANGE/NOWAY/PROTECTIONZONE; the landed code uses the correct 772 `sending.cc` mappings (`DestinationOutOfReach`, `ThereIsNoWay`, `ActionNotPermittedInProtectionZone`) for exact client message parity. **Order fix:** `check_push_destination` runs **before** `tile_query_add_creature` (the TFS-style `queryAdd`) so 772 gates fire with correct error codes — `tile_query_add_creature` was catching MAGICFIELD tiles with `NotPossible` before the 772 AVOID check could return `NotEnoughRoom`. *(August 2026)*

305. **P-D — Per-creature `MovePossible(Execute=true)` push predicates (Gate B, P4)** (`creature/move_possible.rs`, `game_world_player_throw.rs` `check_push_destination`; `operate.cc:515-516` `CheckMapDestination` → `MovingCreature->MovePossible`, `crplayer.cc:363-380` `TPlayer::MovePossible`, `crnonpl.cc:2141-2293` `TMonster::MovePossible`, `crnonpl.cc:1672-1680` `TNPC::MovePossible`, `crmain.cc:883-898` `TCreature::MovePossible`): `CheckMapDestination` calls `MovePossible` on the **moving** creature (not the actor) with `Execute=true, Jump=(OrigZ != DestZ)`. Ported as per-type predicates in `creature/move_possible.rs` dispatching from `check_push_destination`. Key divergences from a naive `-> bool` port: (1) **C2 — signatures are `Result<bool, ReturnValue>`, not `-> bool`.** `TPlayer::MovePossible` **throws** `ENTERPROTECTIONZONE` (`crplayer.cc:366-369`: `EarliestProtectionZoneRound > RoundNr && IsProtectionZone(dest) && !IsProtectionZone(origin)`) and `NOTINVITED` (`crplayer.cc:372-377`: `HouseID != 0 && !IsInvited && !CheckRight(ENTER_HOUSES)`).
