These propagate out of `CheckMapDestination` unchanged — they are **not** remapped to `NOROOM`. `Ok(false)` carries the plain `false` returns (→ `NOROOM` for pushing another creature, `operate.cc:517-518`). Reused the existing self-walk PZ-enter gate mapping (`PlayerIsPzLocked`) and house-invite gate (`houses.is_invited` + `PLAYER_FLAG_CAN_EDIT_HOUSES`). (2) **C3 — the monster kick loop throws `EXHAUSTED`** (`crnonpl.cc:2237` player blocker, `:2240` failed `KickCreature`) and sets `this->Target = 0` before throwing (`crnonpl.cc:2237`). `Err(YouAreExhausted)` with the `Target = 0` side effect applied at the throw site (`clear_targets()`), not by `monster_kick_before_step`. (3) **V1 — `MovePossible(Execute=true)` runs the **full** function during pushes, including the kick loop** (`crnonpl.cc:2185-2288`). The decompile has no "is this a push?" branch — `KickCreature`/`KickBoxes` with retry run for pushes too. Reused `monster_kick_before_step` (already implements the kick loop for self-walk from `on_walk`). Skipping it would let pushes land on blocked tiles that 772 rejects and lose the kick side effects. (4) **V2 — `Radius` is a per-creature spawn-wave field** (`crmain.cc:174`, default `INT_MAX`; set from `Wave->Radius` at `crmain.cc:1886,2061`), **not** a race flag. Added `Monster::radius: i32` (default `i32::MAX`) distinct from `home_radius` (the `MonsterhomeInRange` roam radius). The `Radius` gate (`crnonpl.cc:2154-2159`: `max(|Δx|,|Δy|) > Radius → false`) is skipped when `ATTACKING`/`PANIC`. (5) **Q1 — `GO_STRENGTH` maps to `CreatureBase::speed`** (`creature/base.rs:193`); the 772 `Skills[SKILL_GO_STRENGTH]->Act < 0` check (`crnonpl.cc:2162`) is `base.speed < 0`. NPCs use a separate `NpcMovement::go_strength` (`npcs/mod.rs:69`). (6) **C4 — `crnonpl.cc:2171-2181` is an anti-crowd rule, NOT a leash.** Given `Execute && Master != 0 && State ∉ {ATTACKING, PANIC}` and master on the same z, it rejects when the summon is currently **not** adjacent (manhattan > 1) and the destination **would be** adjacent (≤ 1) — stops a summon from snapping onto the master's tile. Implementing a distance cap (leash) would be wrong. (7) **NPC arm** (`crnonpl.cc:1672-1680`): `BANK && !UNPASS && !AVOID && z==startz && within Radius && !House` — pure boolean, no throws, `Ok(bool)` suffices but keeps `Result` for uniformity. Reuses `NpcRuntimeState::home_position`/`radius` (`creature/npc.rs:54-55`). (8) **Base arm** (`crmain.cc:883-898`): `Jump` → `JumpPossible`; else `BANK && !UNPASS`; `!Execute && AVOID` → false (but `Execute=true` here, so AVOID is handled by Gate C's `tile_has_avoid`). **P-E cleanup:** no dead `is_pushable()` call site remained in `player_push_creature` (P-A already removed it; only a test comment references it); no `D9` markers remained in `creature_todo.rs` (P-B already superseded them); C++ reference headers present on all `move_possible.rs` functions per `TFS-cpp-references.md`. *(August 2026)*

306. **GM `CanPushAllCreatures` push bypass — 772 deviation** (`player/flags.rs` `PLAYER_FLAG_CAN_PUSH_ALL_CREATURES`, `game_world_player_throw.rs` `player_push_creature` / `check_push_destination`, `walk/mod.rs` `FLAG_NOLIMIT`; TFS `const.h:517` `PlayerFlag_CanPushAllCreatures`, `game.cpp:748`): **This is a deliberate deviation from 772 parity, approved by the user.** The 772 decompile has **no** push bypass for GMs — `CheckMoveObject` (`operate.cc:439`), `CheckMapDestination` (`operate.cc:474`), and all three `MovePossible` variants (`crplayer.cc:363`, `crnonpl.cc:2141`, `crnonpl.cc:1672`) never call `CheckRight` for the actor, and the 772 `RIGHT` enum (`enums.hh:455-534`) has no push-related right. A 772 GM is subject to the same push gates as any other player. This feature uses the TFS-domain `PlayerFlag_CanPushAllCreatures` flag (`groups.xml` `canpushallcreatures`) to let access groups bypass **Gate A** (race unpushable, `operate.cc:439`) and **Gate B** (per-creature `MovePossible`, `operate.cc:515-516`) when pushing creatures. **Gate C still applies** — 1-tile range cap (P3), elevation-sum floor-change gate (P5), dest AVOID (P6), PZ→non-PZ, and `ThrowPossible` (P9) all still fire. Implementation: (1) `player_push_creature` computes `can_push_all = player_has_flag(actor, PLAYER_FLAG_CAN_PUSH_ALL_CREATURES)` and skips the Gate A race-unpushable check when set. (2) `check_push_destination` takes `bypass_move_possible: bool` — when true, skips the entire `MovePossible` dispatch (including the monster kick loop) but still runs AVOID and PZ→non-PZ checks. (3) `tile_query_add_creature` is called with `FLAG_NOLIMIT` when `can_push_all` — this bypasses the TFS-domain `queryAdd` checks (PZ, floorchange, teleport, blocking creatures/items) that duplicate 772 `MovePossible` constraints already bypassed. Without this, the `tile_query_add_monster` PZ check (`walk_tile.rs:426`) would re-block what `MovePossible` bypass let through. **Scope note:** TFS 1098's `CanPushAllCreatures` (`game.cpp:748`) only bypasses `isPushable()` (Gate A); this implementation also bypasses Gate B per the user's explicit request ("Gates A + B only"). 8 tests in `push_gm_bypass_tests`: Gate A bypass (unpushable race), Gate B bypass (home range, into PZ, into house), Gate C still enforced (range cap, PZ→non-PZ, dest AVOID), non-GM regression. *(August 2026)*

307. **D4 — Push gates must use the creature's live position, not the packet-time `from_pos`** (`game_world_player_throw.rs` `player_push_creature` / `check_push_destination`; `operate.cc:482` `GetObjectCoordinates(Obj)`, `operate.cc:493-532` `CheckMapDestination`): 772 `CheckMapDestination` reads `Orig*` from `GetObjectCoordinates(Obj)` — the creature's **live** position at execute time, not the packet-time position stored in the `ToDoMove` action. With P-B's 1000 ms `ToDoMove` wait, the target can walk between enqueue and execute. Rust was passing the stale `from_pos` (from `ActionObjectRef.pos`) as the `from` argument to `check_push_destination`, which uses it for the 1-tile range cap (P3), elevation-sum gate (P5), PZ→non-PZ, and `ThrowPossible` (P9). Fix: pass `target_pos` (the live position read from `self.creatures.get(moving_creature).position()`) as `from` instead. In the normal case (creature hasn't moved), `from_pos == target_pos` so the fix is a no-op; it matters when the creature walked during the ToDo wait. The actual move step (`move_creature_on_map`) still uses `from_pos` — this is correct because `internal_get_thing_move` re-resolves the creature from `from_pos` tile, so the creature must be at `from_pos` for the push to reach `player_push_creature` at all. **Test:** `player_push_uses_live_target_position` — sets up a mover at live position (101,103) with stale `from_pos=(101,100)` and dest (101,102). With the fix, the range cap passes (live→dest = 1 tile); without it, `DestinationOutOfReach` (stale→dest = 2 tiles). The dest tile has a `MAGICFIELD` (AVOID) flag so the push fails at the AVOID gate with `NotEnoughRoom` — proving the range cap used the live position. *(August 2026)*

308. **D8 — End-to-end chain-push from `player_push_creature` entry** (`game_world_player_throw.rs` `push_followup_d4_d8_tests`; `crnonpl.cc:2141-2293` `TMonster::MovePossible`, `crnonpl.cc:3066` recursive `MovePossible(Execute=true)`): The F2 chain-push tests (`f2_chain_push_three_monsters` etc.) only exercised the chain from `monster_push_before_step` (the on-walk entry). No test drove the chain from `player_push_creature` — the exact "X - X - X" scenario the user described. Added `player_push_chains_three_monsters`: actor pushes an attacking cyclops (`canPushCreatures`, `Target` set) onto a tile held by a pushable monster which is itself boxed in by a third → chain-push via `player_push_creature` → `check_push_destination` → `monster_move_possible_push` → `monster_kick_before_step` → recursive `monster_kick_creature`. Verifies A ends on dest, B relocated to C's old spot, C relocated to escape, no stacking. Also added `player_push_monster_without_target_is_noroom`: a monster with `Target = 0` does NOT enter the kick loop (`crnonpl.cc:2198`) → `NotEnoughRoom` (NOROOM), no kick side effects. *(August 2026)*

309. **D1 + D2 + D3 — creatures are UNPASS; cross-floor stacking; drop `tile_query_add_creature` from the push path** (`monster_push.rs` `tile_is_bank_and_passable`, `game_world_player_throw.rs` `player_push_creature`; `objects.srv:61-64` TypeID 99 = `{Container,Unpass}`, `operate.cc:1356-1359` `::Move`, `info.cc:702-726` `JumpPossible`): Three interlocked fixes, one commit. **D1** — 772 `objects.srv` TypeID 99 (creature container) has `Flags = {Container,Unpass}`, so any creature on a tile makes `CoordinateFlag(UNPASS)` true. The Rust `tile_is_bank_and_passable` (`CoordinateFlag(BANK) && !CoordinateFlag(UNPASS)`) only scanned items + `BLOCKSOLID` — creatures were never UNPASS. Fix: add `if !body.creatures.is_empty() { return false; }` before the item scan. This makes player/NPC Gate B (`base_move_possible` → `tile_is_bank_and_passable`) correctly return `Ok(false)` → `NOROOM` on occupied tiles, instead of wrongly passing and hitting a late `tile_query_add_creature` block. Also fixes `KickBoxes` dest selection (`monster_kick_single_box` uses `tile_is_bank_and_passable`) treating creature-occupied tiles as free. **D2** — 772 cross-floor push onto an occupied tile: `jump=true` → `JumpPossible(dest, false)` rejects only `UNPASS && UNMOVE`; a creature is `Unpass` but not `Unmove` → **push succeeds, creatures stack**. For a monster cross-floor push, C5 skips `MovePossible` entirely. Rust matched both gate results, then `tile_query_add_creature` blocked on `body.creatures` → `NotEnoughRoom`. **D3** — `tile_query_add_creature` has **no counterpart in the 772 push path** — `operate.cc:1356-1359` runs only `CheckTopMoveObject` / `CheckMoveObject` / `CheckMapDestination` (Gate A/B/C). It was load-bearing only because of D1. Removed the `tile_query_add_creature` call from `player_push_creature` (and the `FLAG_NOLIMIT` GM bypass that went with it). Creature blocking now lives entirely in Gate B via D1's `tile_is_bank_and_passable` fix; cross-floor stacking matches 772 (D2). The GM `CanPushAllCreatures` bypass still works — it skips Gate A + Gate B (`bypass_move_possible`), and Gate C still applies. **Tests:** `player_push_player_onto_occupied_tile_is_noroom` (D1 — same-floor push onto occupied tile → `NotEnoughRoom` from Gate B), `player_push_across_floor_onto_occupied_tile_stacks` (D2 — monster pushed down a floor onto a blocker → `Ok(())`, both creatures stacked on dest). *(August 2026)*
310. **D5 + D6 + D7 — hard-block aborts before KickBoxes; FLOORCHANGE|TELEPORT on push path; kick target gate** (`monster_push.rs` `monster_kick_before_step`, `monster_ai.rs` `monster_move_possible_planning`, `creature/move_possible.rs` `monster_move_possible_push`; `crnonpl.cc:2141-2293` `TMonster::MovePossible`, `crnonpl.cc:2194-2233` creature branch, `crnonpl.cc:2198` `Target == 0`): Three smaller behavioral fixes, one commit. **D5** — 772 `MovePossible` walks the tile stack and `return false`s **immediately** on a creature hard block (target/master/NPC/unpushable/invisible/summon-player/IGNORED), so the item (`KickBoxes`) branch (`crnonpl.cc:2249-2287`, the `else` of `isCreatureContainer()`) is never reached. Rust `break`ed the creature loop on hard blocks but then still ran `monster_kick_boxes`, shoving/destroying boxes on a push that then fails. Fix: `creature_hard_block: bool` flag set on each hard-block `break`; `monster_kick_boxes` gated on `!creature_hard_block`. Only manifests from the `on_walk` entry (`monster_push_before_step` called directly) — the `monster_move_possible_push` path gates hard blocks via `monster_move_possible_planning` before `monster_kick_before_step` is reached. **D6** — `monster_move_possible_planning` rejected `FLOORCHANGE|TELEPORT` tiles, but 772 `TMonster::MovePossible` (`crnonpl.cc:2141-2293`) has no such check (only `PROTECTIONZONE|HOUSE` at `:2168`). Fix: `monster_move_possible_planning` gains `allow_floorchange_teleport: bool`; push path (`monster_move_possible_push`) and kick-chain path (`monster_move_possible_execute_for_kick`) pass `true` (a pushed/chain-kicked monster can land on stairs/teleport in 772); walk path (`monster_tshortway_fill_walkable`, `monster_can_occupy_chase_tile`) keeps `false` (TFS-style stair/teleport avoidance for pathfinding). **D7** — kick-loop target gate was `attack_target.is_some() || follow_target.is_some()`; 772 checks the single `this->Target` (`crnonpl.cc:2198`) = the attack target. A follow-only monster (follow_target set, attack_target None) wrongly entered the kick loop in Rust but not in 772. Fix: `let has_target = target_attack.is_some();`. **Test:** `d5_hard_block_does_not_kick_boxes` (driven from `monster_push_before_step` — unpushable monster + kickable box on dest; box must NOT be kicked when the creature hard block is hit). *(August 2026)*

311. **Furniture AVOID — 772 `Avoid` + `AvoidDamageTypes=0` maps to OTB `blockPathFind`, not `blockSolid`** (`otb.rs` `is_avoid`, `items.rs` `ItemsDB::is_avoid`, `npc/focus.rs` `npc_move_possible`, `monster_ai.rs` `monster_move_possible_planning`, `game_world_player_throw.rs` `tile_has_avoid`, `walk/walk_tile.rs` `tile_query_add_npc` / `tile_query_add_monster`, `monster_push.rs` `item_is_kickable_box`; `crnonpl.cc:1672-1679` `TNPC::MovePossible`, `crnonpl.cc:2141-2293` `TMonster::MovePossible`, `crnonpl.cc:2994-3033` `KickBoxes`, `crmain.cc:883-898` `TCreature::MovePossible`, `enums.hh:240` `AVOID`, `objects.srv` `Avoid` flag): NPCs and monsters were walking through furniture (chairs, boxes, crates, barrels, stairs, trapdoors) because the AVOID flag was mapped to **magic fields only** (`is_avoid_hazard() = is_magic_field() = type_tag == 6`). 772 `objects.srv` has **two** AVOID categories: (a) magic fields (`Avoid` + `AvoidDamageTypes=DAMAGE_FIRE/POISON/ENERGY`) — damaging, race/immunity-gated; (b) furniture/terrain (`Avoid` + `AvoidDamageTypes=0`) — non-damaging, blocks NPC/monster walk but **not** player `Execute=true` (`crmain.cc:893`). The TVP content stack already maps category (b) to OTB `blockPathFind` (bit 2), NOT `blockSolid` (bit 0) — 205 items including chairs, boxes, crates, stairs, trapdoors, holes. **Key insight:** TVP collapses 772 `AVOID`+`UNPASS` into `blockSolid` for its own walk model, but the OTB preserves the distinction — `blockPathFind` for furniture AVOID, `blockSolid` for UNPASS. Using `blockSolid` for furniture would wrongly block players (772 players CAN walk on AVOID furniture at `Execute=true`). **Fix (5 sites + 2 defense-in-depth):** (1) Added `ItemType::is_avoid()` = `is_magic_field() || block_path_find()` and `ItemsDB::is_avoid()` — the full 772 `AVOID` flag. (2) NPC self-walk `npc_move_possible` uses `is_avoid` instead of `is_avoid_hazard` for both ground and item chain entries. (3) Monster planning `monster_move_possible_planning` uses `is_avoid` for items AND ground (ground AVOID covers Bank+Avoid trapdoors/stairs); also fixed the PANIC gate — 772 PANIC ignores **damaging** hazards only (`AvoidDamageTypes != 0`), not furniture (`AvoidDamageTypes=0` → `avoid_damage_type() == None` → never ignored). (4) Push AVOID gate `tile_has_avoid` now scans the tile stack for per-item `is_avoid` (furniture) in addition to the `MAGICFIELD` tile flag (magic fields). (5) `monster_push.rs` `item_is_kickable_box` uses `is_avoid` so `KickBoxes` shoves furniture AVOID items too (`crnonpl.cc:2994-3033` handles both UNPASS and AVOID items); same PANIC `AvoidDamageTypes != 0` fix. (6) Defense-in-depth: `tile_query_add_npc` rejects `BLOCKPATH` tiles; `tile_query_add_monster` treats `BLOCKPATH` like `BLOCKSOLID` (kickable with `can_push_items`, immovable `BLOCKPATH` is a hard block). **Push order note:** 772 `CheckMapDestination` (`operate.cc:515-525`) runs `MovePossible(Execute=true)` (which kicks furniture away) **before** the AVOID re-scan — so a monster with `CanKickBoxes` CAN be pushed onto a furniture tile after kicking the furniture off it. A monster without `CanKickBoxes` is blocked at `MovePossible` (Gate B) before the AVOID scan (Gate C). **No items.xml changes needed** — the OTB already has the correct `blockPathFind` flags for all 205 furniture/terrain items. **Tests:** 9 tests across `npc/tests.rs` and `monster_push_tests.rs` — NPC self-walk blocked by chair, player walk unchanged on chair, push AVOID gate detects furniture, monster plans through furniture with `canPushItems`, monster blocked without `canPushItems`, monster queryAdd blocked/passes with/without `canPushItems`, `item_is_kickable_box` recognizes furniture, PANIC still blocked by furniture. *(August 2026)*

312. **Gap 7a — `register_class` as single owner of TFS class globals** (`crates/tfs-rust-lua/src/class_registry.rs` `register_class` / `register_engine_class_tables`; C++ `luascript.cpp` `LuaScriptInterface::registerClass`): Three mechanisms independently wrote class globals and outcome depended on line order in `LuaRuntime::new`: (1) `userdata/*.rs` set bare **functions** (`globals.set("Tile", tile_new)`) — callable but not indexable, so `function Tile.relocateTo(...)` raised "attempt to index global 'Tile' (a function value)"; (2) `register_event_script_bootstrap` set empty **tables** for a hardcoded 8-name list; (3) hand-written `__call` merge blocks for `Player`/`Creature` only (two ~18-line copies). `Party`/`Teleport`/`Vocation` were never registered (`nil`). Fix: one idempotent `register_class(lua, name, ctor: Option<Function>) -> Table` — get-or-create a table, attach `__call` only if absent. The ctor closure keeps its original `(args...)` signature; `register_class` wraps it so Lua's `__call(self, ...)` drops `self` before forwarding (one place handles the self-drop, not every call site). Table-only classes (`Monster`, `Npc`, `Item`, `Container`, `Party`, `Teleport`, `Vocation`) registered via `register_engine_class_tables`; ctor-bearing classes (`Tile`, `Position`, `Combat`, `ItemType`, `Spell`, `Weapon`, `Player`, `Creature`, `Game`) register themselves. Deleted the 8-name bootstrap list, the two `__call` copies, and the `runtime.rs:139` overwrite comment. **mlua 0.12 API notes:** `Table::get` takes one generic (`get::<Table>(name)`, not `get::<_, Table>`); `Table::metatable()` returns `Option<Table>` directly (not `Result`); `Function::call::<R>(args: impl IntoLuaMulti)` — `MultiValue: IntoLuaMulti` so `f.call::<Value>(rest)` forwards variadic args. **Test gotcha:** `data/lib/core/core.lua` calls `dofile("data/lib/core/storages.lua")` with a CWD-relative path — `lib_core_files_load_with_zero_errors` must `set_current_dir(workspace_root)` (and restore) or it passes only by race with the `global_lua_loads_via_dofile_chain` test's chdir. *(August 2026)*

313. **Gap 7b — shared `__index` chain helper for userdata class-table fallback** (`crates/tfs-rust-lua/src/class_registry.rs` `class_index_lookup` + 8 chain constants; `userdata/{player,tile,item,container,item_type,position,combat,vocation}.rs`; C++ `luascript.cpp` `LuaScriptInterface::registerClass`): Gap 7a made class globals extensible tables (fixing *load*), but `tile:relocateTo(pos)` still errored at *call* time — a Lua method on the class table is not reachable through a userdata unless the userdata's metatable chains `__index` → the class table. Only `CreatureRef` had a fallback, hardcoded to the `Player` table only (`player.rs:800-803`), so all 15 methods in `data/lib/core/creature.lua` (`getPlayer`, `isPlayer`, `setMonsterOutfit`, `addSummon`, `addDamageCondition`, `canAccessPz`, …) plus `functions.lua:530` `Creature:addAttributeCondition` were unreachable from Lua — a latent bug independent of the tools work. Fix: one `class_index_lookup(lua, chain: &'static [&'static str], key: LuaString) -> Result<Value>` free function; each userdata declares its chain as a `pub(crate) const` and adds a one-line `MetaMethod::Index` calling it. Chains: `CreatureRef → [Player, Creature]` (first hit wins), `TileRef → [Tile]`, `ItemRef → [Item]`, `ContainerRef → [Container, Item]` (mirrors C++ `Container extends Item`), `ItemTypeRef → [ItemType]`, `PositionRef → [Position]`, `CombatRef → [Combat]`, `VocationRef → [Vocation]`. **mlua 0.12 priority guarantee:** the generated `__index` (`mlua-0.12.0/src/userdata/util.rs:311-333`) checks field getters → registered methods → the user `__index` function **last**, so native Rust methods keep priority over Lua class-table overrides by construction — no manual ordering to assert (verified by `gap7b_native_method_wins_over_lua_override`). This also means adding `MetaMethod::Index` to `PositionRef` (which has `add_fields` for x/y/z) is safe — field getters are checked first. **No speculative fallbacks:** `Spell`/`Weapon`/`Condition`/`Npc`/`Group`/`MonsterType` userdata have no `function <Class>:method(...)` consumers in `data/`, so they are intentionally not wired (the Gap 7a scope correction: "Nothing in the data pack extends Spell/Weapon/Condition — converting those is speculative work with no consumer. Do not do it."). **`&'static [&'static str]` chain** avoids capture/allocation in the `add_meta_method` closure (closures must be `'static`); a free function + static slice is cleaner than N captured `Vec<String>`s. **Test setup:** `lua.create_userdata(TileRef{...})` + `globals.set("_probe", ud)` builds a live userdata without any `ScriptContext` (the `Tile(x,y,z)` constructor would also work but uniformly covers table-only classes like `Vocation` that have no Lua constructor); then `function <Class>.__gap7b_probe(self) return '<Class>' end; return _probe:__gap7b_probe()` verifies call-time resolution — the exact check that would have caught the 7a-only plan. *(August 2026)*

314. **Gap 7c — revscript ctor globals through `register_class`** (`class_registry.rs` registry recording + `REQUIRED_CLASS_GLOBALS`; `runtime.rs` `Action`/`TalkAction`/`MoveEvent`/`Channel`/`Condition`/`CreatureEvent`/`GlobalEvent`/`Variant`; `userdata/monster_type.rs`; `data/lib/core/create_functions.lua`; C++ `luascript.cpp` `registerClass` / `luaCreateCreatureEvent` / `luaCreateGlobalEvent`): 7a converted userdata classes; revscript constructors were still `globals.set(Name, ctor_fn)`, so `getmetatable(Action)` was nil and `helper_constructors.lua` failed at load (`attempt to index local 'MT'`). `MonsterType` was a function so `register_monster_type.lua` could not assign `.register`. `createFunctions` lived only in `compat.lua`, which decision #3 does not load. Fix: same `register_class` primitive; `CreatureEvent`/`GlobalEvent` are plain tables with `__call` (Action pattern), not userdata — `function creatureevent.onLogin` is a table-field write and does not need compat.lua `__newindex`. **No MonsterType 7b chain:** the only `mType:register(...)` call is `data/monster/lua/#example.lua` (`#` prefix, skipped). **`createFunctions` ported** into `data/lib/core/` rather than loading compat or excluding the scripts/lib file — Gap 5a can be fatal without a special-case skip. **Registry recording** inside `register_class` (name → has `__call`) plus a required-name allowlist: enumerating only the registry would miss a `globals.set` bypass of a known class; the allowlist alone would miss a new `register_class` call. Both together. `NpcType`/`NpcDialogue` still bare functions (no lib consumer). Tests: `all_class_globals_are_tables`, `scripts_lib_files_load_with_zero_failures`. `tools_scripts_load_and_register` now exercises `helper_constructors.lua`'s `__call` wrap around `Action()`. *(August 2026)*

315. **Gap 5a — lib-stage load is fatal and aggregated** (`actions.rs` `load_data_lib` / `exec_lib_stage_files`; `runtime.rs` `LuaError::LibStageFailures`; `run_server.rs`; `combat_scripts.rs` `load_spell_scripts`; C++ `script.cpp` `Scripts::loadScripts` + `scriptmanager.cpp` lib vs content split): Gap 5's 10-name allowlist passed while 9 of 17 `data/lib/core` files (and later 3 of 5 `data/scripts/lib`) failed under warn-and-continue — boot was green, use-time `nil` was the discovery path. After 7a+7c those files load, so the policy could flip without bricking boot. **Fix:** collect every IO/exec error across the three lib scans into one `LibStageFailures(Vec<(path, error)>)` instead of `tracing::warn!` + `Ok(())`. `run_server.rs` `anyhow::bail!`s (same pattern as `assert_required_data_globals`); `load_spell_scripts` propagates instead of swallowing. Content-stage (`load_action_scripts`, per-spell/weapon loops) stays warn-and-continue so a broken shard script cannot brick the server. **Skip `core.lua`/`lib.lua`:** they are `dofile` dispatchers — under a recursive scan they double-loaded 15 core files, and CWD-relative `dofile` failed outside the repo root while the scan still loaded the rest, which is why the dispatcher failure was invisible. Step 11 may replace the scan with the Lua dofile chain; until then skip, don't dual-run. **Tests:** `lib_stage_loads_with_zero_failures` (happy path on the shipped pack) would have passed *before* 5a too (`load_data_lib` always returned `Ok`); `lib_stage_failures_are_fatal_and_aggregated` is the actual policy lock — two `error()` files → one error listing both, dispatchers not reported. *(August 2026)*

316. **Gap 1 — `Action:allowFarUse` + ToDo far-use plumbing** (`runtime.rs` Action ctor; `PendingAction`/`ActionDef`/`ActionEntry`; `EventDispatcher::action_allows_far_use`; `idle_stimulus.rs` Use Obj2 arm; C++ `luaActionAllowFarUse` / `Action::canExecuteAction` / `Actions::canUseFar`): `fishing_rod.lua` failed at load (`allowFarUse` was `nil`). 772 fishing rod (`objects.srv` TypeID 3483) has **no** `DistUse` (only `Take`), so the ToDo arm walked to water without the Lua flag — same class of bug as rune `allowFarUse` (lesson 264). Fix: setter stores `_allow_far_use`; drain through the action registry; Obj2 range uses TFS `areInRange<7,5>` + default `checkFloor` (rune path), not DistUse Chebyshev 7. `blockWalls`/`checkFloor` Lua setters not added (no tools consumer; C++ defaults). Fishing `onUse` still needs Gap 4 (`SKILL_*`) and Gap 3 methods. *(August 2026)*

317. **Gap 4 — `SKILL_*` + TVP `actionIds` in `global.lua`** (`combat_enums.rs` `register_skills`; `data/global.lua`; `actionids.lua`; `inject_door_tables_from_global`): Lua `SKILL_FIST`…`SKILL_LEVEL` are TFS `enums.h` `skills_t` (0–8), **not** 772 timer-skill indices (`SKILL_FED`, `SKILL_GO`, …). `SkillNr` lives in `tfs-rust-core` so lua hardcodes the integers. TVP defines `actionIds` in `data/global.lua` (4000–4005, `destroyableStone=4004`) — there is no TVP `actionids.lua`. Scripts consume the table; they do not register the names (`pick.lua` binds item id 2553 then compares the target's aid). TFS 1.4.2 later split a table into `actionids.lua`; our recursive core scan would **replace** a TVP table if that file assigned `actionIds = {…}`. Fix: inject starts at `actionIds = {`; `actionids.lua` only merges TFS extras (`levelDoor`/`citizenship`) that TVP does not have. *(August 2026)*

318. **Gap 3 — tools Lua verbs** (`game_world_lua_tools.rs`, `userdata/{player,tile,item}.rs`, `runtime.rs` `register_game_api` / `register_do_target_combat`; C++ `luaPlayerAddSkillTries` / `luaPlayerGetEffectiveSkillLevel` / `luaPlayerIsPzLocked` / `luaTileGetBottomCreature` / `luaTileAddItem` / `luaItemGetFluidType` / `luaGameCreateItem` / `luaDoTargetCombat` / `luaTileGetGround`): Nine missing methods plus `getGround` returning Item userdata (it previously wrapped `body.ground` type id as `ItemTypeRef`, so `onUsePick`'s `ground.actionid` / `:transform` could not work even after `Item.actionid`). Mutations apply immediately. `addSkillTries` does **not** multiply by `rateSkill` — TFS applies rate in `Player:onGainSkillTries`, and `data/lib/core/player.lua` sets `APPLY_SKILL_MULTIPLIER = false` around the native. `isPzLocked` is `earliest_protection_zone_round > round_nr`. `getBottomCreature` is the oldest on the tile (`creatures.first()`; TFS `insert(begin)` so `rbegin()` is oldest). `doTargetCombatHealth` is not a C++ symbol — TFS native is `luaDoTargetCombat`; Health is a `compat.lua` alias. Both globals registered; caster `0`/`nil` is environment damage; single-target (must not reuse `CombatExecute` AoE). `Game.createItem` without pos leaves a detached SlotMap item. `Container:addItemEx` still missing. *(August 2026)*

319. **Pillar 5 — LuaLS stubs from the live VM, not a hand inventory** (`lua_defs.rs` / `class_registry.rs` `RecordingRegistry`; `lua-defs/*.d.lua`; `.luarc.json`): mlua's userdata `__index` is a closure, so method names are invisible on the metatable — override `UserData::register` (~15 types) with `register_with_recording` and flush names into the Lua registry the same way `register_class` records class globals. Dummy `create_userdata` in the emitter forces `T::register` without changing empty `register_userdata_type` calls. Colon vs dot: userdata/ctor-instance methods emit `function Tile:getGround(...)`; class-table functions emit `function Game.createItem(...)`. Do **not** dump lib-stage Lua methods into the stubs (`Tile.relocateTo` stays in `data/lib/core/tile.lua` — LuaLS infers it from the workspace). **`--check=.` is mandatory:** `--check=data/lib` sets the workspace to `data/lib`, so `library: ["./lua-defs"]` misses the stubs and every engine global looks undefined. `.luarc.json` `diagnostics.globals` is the known-not-yet-registered TFS surface (`db`, `ITEM_GOLD_COIN`, …); a new missing global still fails CI. Two scripts with undeclared locals (`Obj2`, `creature`) are ignored until those files are fixed. *(August 2026)*

320. **Gap 6 — tool parity numbers in `data/formulas/<era>.lua`** (`formulas.rs` `FishingTuning` / `DestroyableStoneTuning`; `inject_era_formulas`; `pick.lua` / `fishing_rod.lua`): TFS `fishing_rod.lua` used `min(max(10+(skill-10)*0.597,10),50)` — that coefficient is **not** in the 772 decompile. 772 fishing is `moveuse.dat` `TestSkill (User,Fishing,80,50)` → `TSkillProbe::Probe` (`crskill.cc:546`): `skill >= rand()%diff && rand()%100 <= prob` (and `Increase(1)` is already `addSkillTries` in the script). The 0.597 clamp is the 1098/TFS curve (50% at skill 77). Pick 40% / −50 is TVP `actionIds.destroyableStone` content — 772 `BEGIN "Picking"` is `372→394` plus two position-locked quest rocks (`Damage` 40 or 50 on the fail arm), not a generic aid roll. `MOVEUSE_CONDITION_RANDOM` is `random(1,100)<=n`, which the Lua `math.random(1,100)` path already matched. Game VM execs the same era file `load_mechanics` parses (`inject_era_formulas` after lib load). Lesson 316 said TypeID 3483 has no `DistUse`; current `objects.srv` has `{UseEvent,MultiUse,DistUse,Take}` — DistUse is present; `allowFarUse` remains the Lua/TFS far-use contract. *(August 2026)*

321. **Docker is the Rust binary, not C++ `tfs`**: the repo-root `Dockerfile` used to be Forgotten Server Alpine/cmake (`COPY src` + `make`). The Rust server is `tfs-rust`, binds login/game from `config.lua` / `TFS_*` env, and creates schema via SQLx `run_migrations` — Compose must not also `schema.sql` into MariaDB init (duplicate DDL vs baseline migration). `CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR` for `tfs-rust-db` is baked at image compile time and is gone in the slim runtime, so set `TFS_MIGRATIONS_DIR`. `DATABASE_URL` + `TFS_PUBLIC_IP` already override `config.lua` for containers (lesson 5). Do not ignore `/src` without a leading slash in `.dockerignore` — that would drop `crates/*/src`. *(August 2026)*

322. **PropStream strings are Latin-1 bytes, not UTF-8** (`propstream.rs` `write_string` / `read_string`; C++ `PropWriteStream::writeString` / `PropStream::readString`): `read_string` maps each byte to `U+00xx`. `write_string` used `str.as_bytes()` (UTF-8), so `"¡"` (`U+00A1`) wrote `C2 A1` and came back `"Â¡"`. CI `prop_stream_round_trip` (`str_val in ".*"`) failed on that. Write ISO-8859-1 (code points `> U+00FF` → `?`); the proptest strategy is `[\\x00-\\xFF]*`. Wire `NetworkMessage` stays UTF-8 read/write. *(August 2026)*

323. **LuaJIT count hooks require `jit.off()`** (`instruction_budget.rs`; mlua 0.12 `tests/hooks.rs` `test_limit_execution_instructions`): compiled traces never fire `HookTriggers::every_nth_instruction`. A persistent lifetime hook would also spend the budget across the whole process and then kill legitimate scripts — re-arm per Rust→Lua entry, share the outer budget on nested Lua→Rust→Lua. Abort is isolation, not rollback (Mutation Path). `luaInstructionBudget = 0` restores JIT. *(August 2026)*

324. **Game VM stdlib is an allowlist, not mlua `ALL_SAFE`** (`stdlib_allowlist.rs`; pillar 1): `Lua::new()` ships `io` / `os.execute` / `package.loadlib` / `loadstring`. LuaJIT has no `StdLib::COROUTINE` flag (`coroutine` is base). Load `OS` only to copy `time`/`date`/`clock` (strftime + `os.date("*t")` + `os.time{year=…}` must stay LuaJIT-exact — do not reimplement), then replace the table. `JIT` stays for `luaInstructionBudget = 0`. `tfs.appendLog(kind, text)` is the only script write; `kind` is a relative path under `data/logs/` (no `..` / absolute / NUL). Returns `false` on failure, matching `if not file then return`. Command logs keep `data/logs/{name} commands.log`; bug reports move from `data/reports/bugs/` to `data/logs/bugs/` so the jail has one root. `dofile` stays for `global.lua`; `data/migrations/*.lua` are not loaded (SQLx). File appends stay on the game thread — same as TFS `io.open`, small writes. *(August 2026)*

325. **772 `FLUID_*` is sequential 0–12, not TFS colour-mapped subtypes** (`constants.rs` `register_fluids`; TVP `gameserver/src/const.h:94-106`): TFS 1.4.2 `FluidTypes_t` aliases colours (`FLUID_WATER = FLUID_BLUE`, `FLUID_OIL = BROWN+8`, `FLUID_MANA` / `FLUID_LIFE`) so oil is 11 and lemonade is 5. 772 is `FLUID_NONE…LEMONADE` consecutive (`FLUID_OIL=7`, `FLUID_MANAFLUID=10`, `FLUID_LIFEFLUID=11`, `FLUID_LEMONADE=12`). `fluids.lua` / `used_lamp.lua` use the 772 names; registering TFS numbers would fill/drink the wrong liquid. `TALKTYPE_MONSTER_SAY` is `0x11` (772), not TFS 36. `CONDITION_PARAM_DRUNKENNESS=55` is a load-time global only — E8 ignores the value. *(August 2026)*

326. **Action `onUse` no-target is a zero-thing table, not nil, and `isHotkey` is the 6th arg** (`runtime.rs` `call_action_on_use` / `zero_thing_table`; `lua_scope.rs` `fire_on_use_action` / `is_hotkey_use_position`; C++ `Action::executeUse` `callFunction(6)`, `LuaScriptInterface::pushThing` nullptr): passing `nil` crashed scripts that read `target.itemid` or call `target:isItem()`. TFS pushes `{uid=0, itemid=0, actionid=0, type=0}` with **no** Item metatable — `target:isItem()` still errors, so scripts must `type(target) ~= "userdata"` first (`functions.lua` `destroyItem`, `pumpkinhead.lua`, `used_lamp.lua`). `isHotkey` is `(pos.x == 0xFFFF && pos.y == 0 && pos.z == 0)` from the **client** position (`Game::playerUseItem`), not the resolved inventory slot — slot `y=0` would false-positive. Self-use still passes the used item as target (`player_use_item_core`); TFS `internalUseItem` passes nullptr. Changing that is a later behavior change (doors/fluids). *(August 2026)*

327. **Destroy is 1/3 `Change` in place; `fluidsource` names are 772 sequential** (`ItemType::destroyTo` / `fluidSource`; `functions.lua` `destroyItem`; 772 `UseWeapon` `moveuse.cc`): TFS `destroyItem` used `math.random(7)` then `Game.createItem` + `decay` + `remove`. 772 always poffs, then `random(1,3)==1` empties a container and `Change`s the target (`DESTROYTARGET`). Lua `getDestroyId` / `getFluidSource` read typed XML fields. `FluidTypesMap` names (`water`, `mud`, …) map to 772 `FLUID_*` 0–12 (`const.h:94-106`), not TFS colour-mapped ids (`FLUID_OIL=11` vs 772 `7`). Unknown names (TFS extras like `coconut`) stay `FLUID_NONE` and warn. *(August 2026)*

328. **`addHealth` is an HP clamp, not `combatChangeHealth`** (`lua_script_player_add_health`; TFS `luaCreatureAddHealth`; 772 `Heal` `magic.cc:2086`): TFS routes `creature:addHealth` through `combatChangeHealth` (death, damage text, combat params). E4 matches `addMana`: clamp to `[0, effective_max_health]`, `send_player_stats`, and on a real gain `notify_creature_healed` (772 `THealingImpact` health bar, no animated text). 772 `Heal` skips when hitpoints are already 0 (`magic.cc:2107`) — do not resurrect via life fluid. `Heal`/`RefreshMana` emit `EFFECT_MAGIC_BLUE` themselves; the Lua method does not (same as `addMana` vs `RefreshMana`). Paralyze-cancel (`GO_STRENGTH` MDAct) stays on the combat healing path, not this clamp. *(August 2026)*

329. **E5–E7 other-actions — say / spellbook / getHouse** (`lua_mutation.rs` `PlayerSay` / `PlayerShowTextDialog`; `userdata/{player,tile}.rs`; `spellbook.lua`; `construction_kits.lua`; 772 `Talk`, `GetSpellbook` `magic.cc:3830-3901`, `GetSpellString` `magic.cc:3780`, `IsHouse` `map.cc:2474`):
    - **`Player:say` is viewport talk, not the chat parser.** `player_say` runs `playerSaySpell`. Fluids `"Mmmh."` / `"Gulp."` must call `broadcast_creature_say_viewport` (TFS `internalCreatureSay`). Default type is `TALKTYPE_SAY = 1`.
    - **Spellbook is `SpellKnown`, not TFS `canCast`.** `Game.getInstantSpells()` lists every instant def (incl. rune-conjure). Filter `level > 0` and `hasLearnedSpell` (`persist.spells`, case-insensitive). Words: strip commas, glue the first two syllables (`ex,ura` → `exura`; `ex,ura, gran` → `exura gran`). Line `"  " .. words .. " - " .. name .. ": " .. mana`. Specials by name: Summon/Convince `"var"`, Berserk `"4*Level"` (not `80%`). Skip level-0 GM/house. OTB **2175 only** — 2217 is fontsize-1 stored text. `showTextDialog` is `0x96` `send_text_window_simple_item`.
    - **`Tile:getHouse()` is `nil` or House userdata, never `0`.** Lua `0` is truthy, so a numeric miss would unpack kits on the street. 772 `IsHouse` is `GetHouseID != 0`. House tile: `transform` + effect 3; else effect 4, no text.
    - **Lua mutation tests cannot capture with closures.** `LuaMutationApplier` is `fn` (`OnceLock`); capturing closures do not coerce. Lua-crate tests use a thread-local applier override plus a non-capturing `fn` that writes a `thread_local` cell. *(August 2026)*

330. **E8–E9 drunk stack vs TFS ticks; inject `getFormattedWorldTime` not full `global.lua`** (`condition.rs` `apply_drink_drunk_stack` / `tick_drunk_skill`; `process_skills.rs`; `fluids.lua`; `actions.rs` inject; `watch.lua`; 772 `moveuse.cc:1776-1782`, `crskill.cc:176-193`):
    - **Beer/wine is Cycle 1–5 × Count=120, not TFS 60s + `CONDITION_PARAM_DRUNKENNESS`.** Lua `addCondition(CONDITION_DRUNK)` ignores ticks and param 55. If Cycle `< 5`, `SetTimer(level+1, 120, 120)`. At cap 5, Count is **not** refreshed. `base.drunkenness` is Cycle (`TimerValue`). Each ProcessSkills: Count--; on 0, Cycle toward 0, Count=MaxCount; at 0 remove. Plan/test is 120 rounds to step (decrement-then-Event), not C++'s extra tick where Event runs when Count is already 0 at tick start.
    - **Spell-drunk stays Power-gated** (`idle_stimulus` `Power >= TimerValue`). Same `TSkill::Process`; Duration is MaxCount so Cycle steps down every Duration seconds (not TFS snap-to-zero when ticks elapse).
    - **`fluids.lua` is `UseLiquidContainer`.** Fill → pour → drink iff dest is self (or hotkey) → else spill `2016`. Mana `50..=150`, life `25..=75`. No magic-blue, no exhaust, no `queryAdd`.
    - **E9 injects `getFormattedWorldTime` only.** Full `dofile('data/global.lua')` would double-load `lib.lua`. Watch ids: pendulum 1728–1731, 2036, cuckoo 1873–1877 and 1881; drop sundial 3900. *(August 2026)*
